```
1→"""Preflight guards for subjective review reruns.
     2→
     3→Blocks reruns while backlog remains (objective findings and/or subjective queue
     4→work), unless ``--force-review-rerun`` is set. Also clears stale subjective
     5→markers before a new cycle.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import re
    11→import sys
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import CommandError
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_subjective_visibility, load_plan
    16→from desloppify.state import StateModel, save_state
    17→
    18→from .helpers import parse_dimensions
    19→
    20→
    21→def clear_stale_subjective_entries(
    22→    state: StateModel,
    23→    *,
    24→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    25→) -> list[str]:
    26→    """Clear ``needs_review_refresh`` markers from subjective assessments.
    27→
    28→    When *dimensions* is provided, only those dimensions are cleared;
    29→    otherwise all stale dimensions are cleared.
    30→
    31→    Returns the list of dimension keys that were cleared.
    32→    """
    33→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    34→    cleared: list[str] = []
    35→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    36→        if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
    37→            continue
    38→        if dimensions is not None and dim_key not in dimensions:
    39→            continue
    40→        if assessment.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    41→            assessment.pop("needs_review_refresh", None)
    42→            assessment.pop("stale_since", None)
    43→            assessment.pop("refresh_reason", None)
    44→            cleared.append(dim_key)
    45→    return cleared
    46→
    47→
    48→def _scored_dimensions(state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    49→    """Return dimension keys that already have a nonzero subjective score."""
    50→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    51→    scored: list[str] = []
    52→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    53→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
    54→            if assessment.get("score", 0):
    55→                scored.append(dim_key)
    56→        elif isinstance(assessment, (int, float)) and assessment:
    57→            scored.append(dim_key)
    58→    return sorted(scored)
    59→
    60→
    61→def _normalize_dimension_key(raw: object) -> str:
    62→    """Normalize a dimension key/name to canonical snake_case."""
    63→    text = str(raw or "").strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    64→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text).strip("_")
    65→
    66→
    67→
    68→def _blocking_scored_dimensions(
    69→    state: StateModel,
    70→    *,
    71→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
    72→    normalized_dimensions: set[str] | None,
    73→) -> list[str]:
    74→    scored_dims = _scored_dimensions(state)
    75→    if dimensions is None:
    76→        return scored_dims
    77→    normalized = normalized_dimensions or set()
    78→    return sorted(
    79→        dim
    80→        for dim in scored_dims
    81→        if _normalize_dimension_key(dim) in normalized
    82→    )
    83→
    84→
    85→def _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
    86→    state: StateModel,
    87→    *,
    88→    blocking_dims: list[str],
    89→) -> tuple[int, int]:
    90→    plan_data = load_plan()
    91→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, plan=plan_data)
    92→    objective_total = policy.objective_count
    93→    # Subjective dimensions are resolved BY running reviews, so they never
    94→    # block review --prepare (that would be circular).  Only objective
    95→    # findings constitute genuine blocking backlog.
    96→    return objective_total, 0
    97→
    98→
    99→def _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   100→    *,
   101→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   102→    objective_total: int,
   103→    subjective_total: int,
   104→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   105→) -> None:
   106→    print(
   107→        colorize(
   108→            "  Blocked: rerun requires drained backlog "
   109→            f"(objective: {objective_total}, subjective: {subjective_total}).",
   110→            "red",
   111→        ),
   112→        file=sys.stderr,
   113→    )
   114→    print(
   115→        colorize(
   116→            f"  Scored dimensions: {', '.join(blocking_dims)}",
   117→            "yellow",
   118→        ),
   119→        file=sys.stderr,
   120→    )
   121→    if objective_total > 0:
   122→        print(
   123→            colorize(
   124→                f"  Open objective finding(s): {objective_total}",
   125→                "yellow",
   126→            ),
   127→            file=sys.stderr,
   128→        )
   129→    if subjective_total > 0:
   130→        print(
   131→            colorize(
   132→                f"  Open subjective queue item(s): {subjective_total}",
   133→                "yellow",
   134→            ),
   135→            file=sys.stderr,
   136→        )
   137→    print("", file=sys.stderr)
   138→    if dimensions is not None:
   139→        unscored = sorted(dimensions - set(blocking_dims))
   140→        if unscored:
   141→            print(
   142→                colorize(
   143→                    f"  Tip: target only unscored dimensions with "
   144→                    f"--dimensions {','.join(unscored)}",
   145→                    "dim",
   146→                ),
   147→                file=sys.stderr,
   148→            )
   149→    print(
   150→        colorize(
   151→            "  Resolve open items first, or override with --force-review-rerun",
   152→            "dim",
   153→        ),
   154→        file=sys.stderr,
   155→    )
   156→
   157→
   158→def review_rerun_preflight(
   159→    state: StateModel,
   160→    args,
   161→    *,
   162→    state_file=None,
   163→    save_fn=save_state,
   164→) -> None:
   165→    """Single entry point: gate check -> clear stale -> save.
   166→
   167→    Exits with code 1 when open backlog exists and ``--force-review-rerun`` is
   168→    not set. On success, clears stale subjective markers for targeted
   169→    dimensions and persists state.
   170→    """
   171→    dimensions = parse_dimensions(args)
   172→    normalized_dimensions = (
   173→        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
   174→        if dimensions is not None
   175→        else None
   176→    )
   177→
   178→    # --force-review-rerun bypasses the gate
   179→    if getattr(args, "force_review_rerun", False):
   180→        print(
   181→            colorize(
   182→                "  --force-review-rerun: bypassing rerun backlog checks.",
   183→                "yellow",
   184→            )
   185→        )
   186→    else:
   187→        # Only gate dimensions that are actually targeted by this review run.
   188→        blocking_dims = _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   189→            state,
   190→            dimensions=dimensions,
   191→            normalized_dimensions=normalized_dimensions,
   192→        )
   193→
   194→        # No gate when none of the targeted dimensions have prior scores —
   195→        # this is a first run for these dimensions, not a rerun.
   196→        if blocking_dims:
   197→            objective_total, subjective_total = _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   198→                state,
   199→                blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   200→            )
   201→            if objective_total > 0 or subjective_total > 0:
   202→                _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   203→                    blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   204→                    objective_total=objective_total,
   205→                    subjective_total=subjective_total,
   206→                    dimensions=dimensions,
   207→                )
   208→                raise CommandError(
   209→                    f"rerun blocked: open backlog (objective: {objective_total}, "
   210→                    f"subjective: {subjective_total})",
   211→                    exit_code=1,
   212→                )
   213→
   214→    # Gate passed — clear stale for targeted dims
   215→    cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, dimensions=dimensions)
   216→    if cleared and state_file:
   217→        save_fn(state, state_file)
   218→        print(
   219→            colorize(
   220→                f"  Cleared stale review markers: {', '.join(sorted(cleared))}",
   221→                "cyan",
   222→            )
   223→        )
   224→
```

```
1→"""Target-score normalization helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.text_api import is_numeric
     6→
     7→
     8→def coerce_target_score(value: object, *, fallback: float = 95.0) -> float:
     9→    """Normalize target score-like values to a safe [0, 100] float."""
    10→    if is_numeric(fallback):
    11→        fallback_value = float(fallback)
    12→    else:
    13→        fallback_value = 95.0
    14→
    15→    if is_numeric(value):
    16→        parsed = float(value)
    17→    elif isinstance(value, str):
    18→        text = value.strip()
    19→        if not text:
    20→            parsed = fallback_value
    21→        else:
    22→            try:
    23→                parsed = float(text)
    24→            except ValueError:
    25→                parsed = fallback_value
    26→    else:
    27→        parsed = fallback_value
    28→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, parsed))
    29→
    30→
    31→def target_strict_score_from_config(
    32→    config: dict | None, *, fallback: float = 95.0
    33→) -> float:
    34→    """Read and normalize target strict score from config."""
    35→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    36→        raw = config.get("target_strict_score", fallback)
    37→    else:
    38→        raw = fallback
    39→    return coerce_target_score(raw, fallback=fallback)
    40→
    41→
```

```
1→"""Runtime context helpers for command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    11→from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    12→
    13→
    14→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    15→class CommandRuntime:
    16→    """Explicit runtime dependencies shared by command handlers."""
    17→
    18→    config: dict[str, Any]
    19→    state: dict[str, Any]
    20→    state_path: Path | None
    21→
    22→
    23→def command_runtime(args) -> CommandRuntime:
    24→    """Return runtime context from explicit args.runtime or construct one."""
    25→    runtime = getattr(args, "runtime", None)
    26→    if isinstance(runtime, CommandRuntime):
    27→        return runtime
    28→
    29→    config = load_config()
    30→    state_file = state_path(args)
    31→    if isinstance(state_file, str):
    32→        state_file = Path(state_file)
    33→
    34→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    35→
    36→    return CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
    37→
    38→
    39→__all__ = ["CommandRuntime", "command_runtime"]
    40→
```

```
1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import is_subjective_queue_item
    10→
    11→
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→
    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class QueueBreakdown:
    18→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    19→
    20→    queue_total: int = 0
    21→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    22→    skipped: int = 0
    23→    subjective: int = 0
    24→    workflow: int = 0
    25→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    26→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    27→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    28→
    29→    @property
    30→    def actionable(self) -> int:
    31→        """Items that represent real work — excludes workflow navigation steps.
    32→
    33→        Use this instead of ``queue_total`` when gating on "is there work left?"
    34→        (e.g. scan preflight, plan-start-score clearing).  Workflow items like
    35→        ``workflow::run-scan`` guide the user but are not work to complete.
    36→        """
    37→        return max(0, self.queue_total - self.workflow)
    38→
    39→
    40→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    41→    state: dict,
    42→    plan: dict | None = None,
    43→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    44→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call."""
    45→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    46→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    47→
    48→    result = build_work_queue(
    49→        state,
    50→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    51→            status="open",
    52→            count=None,
    53→            plan=plan,
    54→            include_skipped=False,
    55→        ),
    56→    )
    57→
    58→    # Collapse clusters for display-level counting
    59→    items = result.get("items", [])
    60→    if plan and not plan.get("active_cluster"):
    61→        items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
    62→
    63→    queue_total = len(items)
    64→
    65→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
    66→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
    67→    subjective = sum(
    68→        1 for item in items
    69→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    70→    )
    71→    workflow = sum(
    72→        1 for item in items
    73→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    74→    )
    75→
    76→    # Plan-derived counts
    77→    plan_ordered = 0
    78→    skipped = 0
    79→    if plan:
    80→        skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    81→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
    82→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    83→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    84→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    85→
    86→    # Focus cluster info
    87→    focus_cluster = None
    88→    focus_cluster_count = 0
    89→    focus_cluster_total = 0
    90→    if plan:
    91→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    92→        if active:
    93→            focus_cluster = active
    94→            cluster_data = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    95→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    96→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
    97→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            open_findings = {
    99→                fid
   100→                for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   101→                if f.get("status") == "open"
   102→            }
   103→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_findings)
   104→
   105→    return QueueBreakdown(
   106→        queue_total=queue_total,
   107→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
   108→        skipped=skipped,
   109→        subjective=subjective,
   110→        workflow=workflow,
   111→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
   112→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
   113→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   114→    )
   115→
   116→
   117→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
   119→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   120→
   121→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   122→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   123→
   124→    Examples::
   125→
   126→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   127→        Queue: 1934 items
   128→    """
   129→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   130→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   131→
   132→    # Parenthesized segments
   133→    segments: list[str] = []
   134→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   135→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.workflow} planning step{'s' if breakdown.workflow != 1 else ''}")
   136→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   137→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   138→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   139→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   140→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   141→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   142→    if segments:
   143→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   144→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   145→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   146→    return label
   147→
   148→
   149→def format_queue_block(
   150→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   151→    *,
   152→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   153→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   154→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   155→
   156→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   157→    """
   158→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   159→
   160→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   161→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   162→        focus_line = (
   163→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   164→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   165→            f" items remaining"
   166→        )
   167→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   168→
   169→    # Frozen score line
   170→    if frozen_score is not None:
   171→        lines.append((
   172→            f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   173→            "cyan",
   174→        ))
   175→
   176→    # Queue headline — always the same
   177→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   178→
   179→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   180→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   181→        lines.append((
   182→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   183→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   184→            "dim",
   185→        ))
   186→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   187→        lines.append((
   188→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   189→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   190→            "dim",
   191→        ))
   192→    else:
   193→        lines.append((
   194→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   195→            "dim",
   196→        ))
   197→
   198→    return lines
   199→
   200→
   201→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   202→# Legacy / backward-compatible helpers
   203→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   204→
   205→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
   206→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed).
   207→
   208→    .. deprecated::
   209→        Returns ``queue_total`` which **includes** workflow navigation items.
   210→        Prefer ``plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan).actionable`` for
   211→        gate/preflight checks where workflow items should not count.
   212→    """
   213→    return plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan).queue_total
   214→
   215→
   216→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   217→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   218→    if not plan:
   219→        return None
   220→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   221→
   222→
   223→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   224→    plan: dict,
   225→    queue_remaining: int,
   226→    *,
   227→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None,
   228→) -> None:
   229→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   230→
   231→    When *breakdown* is provided, uses the standardized queue block.
   232→    Otherwise falls back to the legacy two-line format.
   233→    """
   234→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   235→    strict = scores.get("strict")
   236→    if strict is None:
   237→        return
   238→
   239→    if breakdown is not None:
   240→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=strict)
   241→        print()
   242→        for text, style in block:
   243→            print(colorize(text, style))
   244→        if queue_remaining > 0:
   245→            print(colorize(
   246→                "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   247→                "dim",
   248→            ))
   249→        return
   250→
   251→    # Legacy fallback
   252→    print(
   253→        colorize(
   254→            f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {strict:.1f}/100",
   255→            "cyan",
   256→        )
   257→    )
   258→    print(
   259→        colorize(
   260→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
   261→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   262→            "dim",
   263→        )
   264→    )
   265→
   266→
   267→def _print_objective_drained_banner(
   268→    frozen_strict: float,
   269→    remaining: int,
   270→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   271→) -> None:
   272→    """Show a phase-transition banner when objective work is drained."""
   273→    kind_labels: list[str] = []
   274→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   275→        kind_labels.append("subjective")
   276→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   277→        kind_labels.append("workflow")
   278→    kind_desc = " + ".join(kind_labels) if kind_labels else "non-objective"
   279→    print(colorize(
   280→        f"\n  Objective queue complete (plan-start was {frozen_strict:.1f})."
   281→        f" {remaining} {kind_desc} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''} remain.",
   282→        "cyan",
   283→    ))
   284→    print(colorize(
   285→        "  Run `desloppify next` for remaining work,"
   286→        " then `desloppify scan` to finalize.",
   287→        "dim",
   288→    ))
   289→
   290→
   291→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   292→    state: dict,
   293→    prev,
   294→    plan: dict | None,
   295→) -> None:
   296→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
   297→
   298→    Three display modes:
   299→
   300→    1. **Frozen** — objective queue items remain → show frozen plan-start score.
   301→    2. **Phase transition** — objective drained, subjective/workflow remains
   302→       → show live scores + transition banner.
   303→    3. **Live** — no active plan cycle or queue is clear → show live scores.
   304→    """
   305→    frozen_strict: float | None = None
   306→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None
   307→
   308→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
   309→        frozen_strict = plan["plan_start_scores"]["strict"]
   310→        try:
   311→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   312→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   313→            pass
   314→
   315→    remaining = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   316→
   317→    # Frozen: objective work remains — show frozen plan-start score and return
   318→    if remaining > 0 and frozen_strict is not None:
   319→        objective_remaining = remaining - breakdown.subjective - breakdown.workflow
   320→        if objective_remaining > 0:
   321→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   322→                plan, remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   323→            )
   324→            return
   325→
   326→    # Live scores (or phase transition): show current scores
   327→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
   328→
   329→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   330→
   331→    # Phase transition: objective drained but subjective/workflow remains
   332→    if remaining > 0 and frozen_strict is not None:
   333→        _print_objective_drained_banner(frozen_strict, remaining, breakdown)
   334→
   335→
   336→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   337→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   338→
   339→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   340→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   341→    """
   342→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   343→
   344→    try:
   345→        plan = load_plan()
   346→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   347→        plan = None
   348→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   349→
   350→
   351→__all__ = [
   352→    "QueueBreakdown",
   353→    "format_queue_block",
   354→    "format_queue_headline",
   355→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   356→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   357→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
   358→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   359→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   360→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   361→]
   362→
```

```
1→"""Queue-order enforcement helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
     9→
    10→
    11→def _check_queue_order_guard(
    12→    state: dict,
    13→    patterns: list[str],
    14→    status: str,
    15→) -> bool:
    16→    """Warn and block if resolving items not at the front of the plan queue."""
    17→    if status != "fixed":
    18→        return False
    19→    try:
    20→        if not has_living_plan():
    21→            return False
    22→        plan = load_plan()
    23→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    24→        if not queue_order:
    25→            return False
    26→
    27→        result = build_work_queue(
    28→            state,
    29→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
    30→                plan=plan,
    31→                count=None,
    32→                include_subjective=True,
    33→            ),
    34→        )
    35→        items = result["items"]
    36→        if not plan.get("active_cluster"):
    37→            items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
    38→        if not items:
    39→            return False
    40→
    41→        front_item = items[0]
    42→        front_id = front_item["id"]
    43→
    44→        front_ids: set[str] = set()
    45→        if front_item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    46→            for member in front_item.get("members", []):
    47→                front_ids.add(member["id"])
    48→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    49→        else:
    50→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    51→
    52→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    53→        resolved_ids: set[str] = set()
    54→        for pattern in patterns:
    55→            if pattern in clusters:
    56→                resolved_ids.update(clusters[pattern].get("finding_ids", []))
    57→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    58→            else:
    59→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    60→
    61→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
    62→        resolved_ids = {
    63→            finding_id
    64→            for finding_id in resolved_ids
    65→            if finding_id in clusters
    66→            or (finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open")
    67→        }
    68→        if not resolved_ids:
    69→            return False
    70→
    71→        out_of_order = resolved_ids - front_ids
    72→        for cluster_id in list(out_of_order):
    73→            if cluster_id in clusters:
    74→                alive_members = {
    75→                    finding_id
    76→                    for finding_id in clusters[cluster_id].get("finding_ids", [])
    77→                    if finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open"
    78→                }
    79→                if alive_members and alive_members <= front_ids:
    80→                    out_of_order.discard(cluster_id)
    81→        if not out_of_order:
    82→            return False
    83→
    84→        print(colorize("\n  Queue order violation: these items are not next in the plan queue:\n", "yellow"))
    85→        for finding_id in sorted(out_of_order):
    86→            print(f"    {finding_id}")
    87→        print(colorize(f"\n  The current next item is: {front_id}", "dim"))
    88→        print(colorize("  Items must be resolved in plan order. If you need to reprioritize:", "dim"))
    89→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan move <pattern> --position top    # move to front", "dim"))
    90→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan skip <pattern> --reason '...'    # skip for now", "dim"))
    91→        print(colorize("    desloppify next                                  # see what's next\n", "dim"))
    92→        return True
    93→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    94→        return False
    95→
    96→
    97→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard"]
    98→
```

```
    27→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    28→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    29→    detail = item.get("detail")
    30→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    34→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    38→    detector = item.get("detector")
    39→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    40→        return True
    41→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    42→        return True
    43→    return False
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    47→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    48→
    49→
    50→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    51→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    52→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    53→        return True
    54→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    55→        members = item.get("members", [])
    56→        return bool(members) and all(
    57→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    58→        )
    59→    return False
    60→
    61→
    62→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    63→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    64→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    65→    weight_by_confidence = {

    67→        "medium": 0.7,

    69→    }
    70→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    71→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    72→        weight *= 10.0
    73→    return float(weight)
    74→
    75→
    76→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
```

```
1→"""Shared triage guardrail helpers for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.display import short_finding_id
     8→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS, CommandError
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    11→    compute_new_finding_ids,
    12→    is_triage_stale,
    13→    load_plan,
    14→    triage_phase_banner,
    15→)
    16→
    17→
    18→@dataclass
    19→class TriageGuardrailResult:
    20→    """Structured result from triage staleness detection."""
    21→
    22→    is_stale: bool = False
    23→    new_ids: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    24→    _plan: dict | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    25→
    26→
    27→def triage_guardrail_status(
    28→    *,
    29→    plan: dict | None = None,
    30→    state: dict | None = None,
    31→) -> TriageGuardrailResult:
    32→    """Pure detection: is triage stale? Returns structured result, no side effects."""
    33→    try:
    34→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    35→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    36→        return TriageGuardrailResult()
    37→
    38→    resolved_state = state or {}
    39→
    40→    if not is_triage_stale(resolved_plan, resolved_state):
    41→        return TriageGuardrailResult(_plan=resolved_plan)
    42→
    43→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
    44→    if resolved_state:
    45→        new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(resolved_plan, resolved_state)
    46→
    47→    return TriageGuardrailResult(is_stale=True, new_ids=new_ids, _plan=resolved_plan)
    48→
    49→
    50→def print_triage_guardrail_info(
    51→    *,
    52→    plan: dict | None = None,
    53→    state: dict | None = None,
    54→) -> bool:
    55→    """Print yellow info banner if triage is stale. Returns True if banner was shown."""
    56→    result = triage_guardrail_status(plan=plan, state=state)
    57→    if not result.is_stale:
    58→        return False
    59→
    60→    if result.new_ids:
    61→        print(colorize(
    62→            f"  {len(result.new_ids)} new review finding(s) not yet triaged.",
    63→            "yellow",
    64→        ))
    65→
    66→    if result._plan is not None:
    67→        banner = triage_phase_banner(result._plan)
    68→        if banner:
    69→            print(colorize(f"  {banner}", "yellow"))
    70→
    71→    return True
    72→
    73→
    74→def require_triage_current_or_exit(
    75→    *,
    76→    state: dict,
    77→    bypass: bool = False,
    78→    attest: str = "",
    79→) -> None:
    80→    """Gate: exit(1) if triage is stale and not bypassed. Name signals the exit."""
    81→    result = triage_guardrail_status(state=state)
    82→    if not result.is_stale:
    83→        return
    84→
    85→    if bypass and attest and len(attest.strip()) >= 30:
    86→        print(colorize(
    87→            "  Triage guardrail bypassed with attestation.",
    88→            "yellow",
    89→        ))
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    new_ids = result.new_ids
    93→    lines = [
    94→        f"BLOCKED: {len(new_ids) or 'some'} new review finding(s) have not been triaged."
    95→    ]
    96→    if new_ids:
    97→        for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
    98→            f = state.get("findings", {}).get(fid, {})
    99→            lines.append(f"    * [{short_finding_id(fid)}] {f.get('summary', '')}")
   100→        if len(new_ids) > 5:
   101→            lines.append(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more")
   102→    lines.append("")
   103→    lines.append("  NEXT STEP: desloppify plan triage")
   104→    lines.append("  (Review new findings, then either --confirm-existing or re-plan.)")
   105→    lines.append("")
   106→    lines.append("  View new items:  desloppify plan queue --sort recent")
   107→    lines.append('  To bypass: --force-resolve --attest "I understand the plan may be stale..."')
   108→    raise CommandError("\n".join(lines))
   109→
```

```
    44→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    45→class QueueBuildOptions:
    46→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    47→
    48→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    49→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    50→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    51→    """
    52→
    53→    count: int | None = 1
    54→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    55→    scope: str | None = None
    56→    status: str = "open"
    57→    include_subjective: bool = True
    58→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    59→    chronic: bool = False
    60→    explain: bool = False
    61→    plan: dict | None = None
    62→    include_skipped: bool = False
    63→    cluster: str | None = None
    64→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None

    67→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    68→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    69→
    70→    items: list[dict]
    71→    total: int
    72→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    73→    new_ids: set[str]

    75→
    76→def build_work_queue(
    77→    state: StateModel,
    78→    *,
    79→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    80→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    81→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    82→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    83→
    84→    # Resolve scan_path: sentinel → read from state, explicit value → use as-is
    85→    scan_path: str | None = (
    86→        state.get("scan_path")
    87→        if resolved_options.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    88→        else resolved_options.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
    89→    )
    90→
    91→    status = resolved_options.status
    92→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    93→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    94→    try:
    95→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    96→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    97→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    98→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    99→
   100→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   101→        state,
   102→        scan_path=scan_path,
   103→        status_filter=status,
   104→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   105→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   109→
   110→    # Resolve subjective visibility policy for gating.
   111→    # compute_subjective_visibility auto-reads scan_path from state, matching
   112→    # the scan_path we resolved above (both read state["scan_path"]).
   113→    policy = resolved_options.policy or compute_subjective_visibility(
   114→        state,
   115→        plan=resolved_options.plan,
   116→    )
   117→
   118→    if (
   119→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   120→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   121→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   122→    ):
   123→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   124→            state,
   125→            state.get("findings", {}),
   126→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   127→        )
   128→        # When a plan explicitly includes a subjective item in queue_order,
   129→        # surface it regardless of policy — the plan is authoritative.
   130→        plan_queue_set: set[str] = (
   131→            set(resolved_options.plan.get("queue_order", []))
   132→            if resolved_options.plan
   133→            else set()
   134→        )
   135→        for item in subjective_items:
   136→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   137→                continue
   138→            # Non-initial subjective items only surface when objective
   139→            # backlog is drained — unless the plan explicitly queued them
   140→            # (e.g. stale dims injected after a completed cycle).
   141→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   142→            if not policy.should_surface(item) and item_id not in plan_queue_set:
   143→                continue
   144→            all_items.append(item)
   145→
   146→    # Inject triage stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   147→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   148→        synth_items = build_triage_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   149→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   150→        checkpoint_item = build_score_checkpoint_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   151→        if checkpoint_item is not None:
   152→            all_items.append(checkpoint_item)
   153→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   154→        if plan_item is not None:
   155→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   156→
   157→    enrich_with_impact(all_items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   158→
   159→    # Impact floor: drop mechanical findings with negligible score impact
   160→    MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   161→    all_items = [
   162→        item for item in all_items
   163→        if item.get("kind") != "finding"
   164→        or item.get("is_review")
   165→        or item.get("is_subjective")
   166→        or not item.get("estimated_impact")
   167→        or item["estimated_impact"] >= MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   168→    ]
   169→
   170→    # Plan-aware pipeline: enrich, stamp sort keys, separate skipped,
   171→    # then a single sort produces the correct ordering.
   172→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   173→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   174→    if resolved_options.plan:
   175→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   176→        _enrich_plan_metadata(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   177→        _stamp_plan_sort_keys(all_items, resolved_options.plan, new_ids)
   178→        all_items, skipped_items = _separate_skipped(
   179→            all_items, resolved_options.plan,
   180→        )
   181→
   182→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   183→
   184→    if resolved_options.plan:
   185→        if resolved_options.include_skipped:
   186→            all_items = all_items + skipped_items
   187→        _stamp_positions(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   188→        all_items = _filter_cluster_focus(
   189→            all_items, resolved_options.plan, resolved_options.cluster,
   190→        )
   191→
   192→    # Inject "run scan" workflow item when queue is empty and a plan cycle
   193→    # is active (plan_start_scores exist).  This gives the user an explicit
   194→    # next-step rather than a bare "nothing to do" message.
   195→    if not all_items and resolved_options.plan:
   196→        plan_scores = resolved_options.plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   197→        if plan_scores.get("strict") is not None:
   198→            all_items.append({
   199→                "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   200→                "kind": "workflow_action",
   201→                "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   202→                "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   203→                "file": "",
   204→                "detector": "workflow",
   205→                "confidence": "high",
   206→            })
   207→
   208→    total = len(all_items)
   209→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   210→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   211→
   212→    if resolved_options.explain:
   213→        for item in all_items:
   214→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   215→
   216→    return {
   217→        "items": all_items,
   218→        "total": total,
   219→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   220→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   221→    }
   222→
   223→
   224→__all__ = [

   227→    "WorkQueueResult",
   228→    "build_work_queue",

   231→]
```

```
   120→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   121→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   122→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   123→    target = cleaned.lower()
   124→
   125→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   126→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   127→            return str(dim_key)
   128→    return slugify(cleaned)

   130→
   131→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   132→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   133→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   134→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   135→    return {
   136→        cleaned.lower(),
   137→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   138→        slugify(cleaned),
   139→        canonical.lower(),
   140→        slugify(canonical),
   141→    }

   144→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   145→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   146→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   147→    if not lang:
   148→        return None
   149→
   150→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   151→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   152→        return None
   153→
   154→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   155→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   156→        return None
   157→
   158→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   159→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   160→        return None
   161→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   162→
   163→
   164→def primary_command_for_finding(
   165→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   166→) -> str:
   167→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   168→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   169→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   170→        available_fixers = [
   171→            fixer
   172→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   173→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   174→        ]
   175→        if available_fixers:
   176→            return f"desloppify autofix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   177→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   178→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   179→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   180→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   181→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'

   184→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   185→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   186→
   187→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   188→    """

   191→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,

   194→        TRIAGE_IDS,
   195→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   196→    )
   197→
   198→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   199→    order_set = set(order)
   200→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   201→    if not present:
   202→        return []
   203→
   204→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   205→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   206→
   207→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   208→    open_review_count = sum(
   209→        1 for f in findings.values()
   210→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   211→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   212→    )
   213→
   214→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   215→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   216→
   217→    items: list[dict] = []
   218→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   219→        if sid not in present:
   220→            continue
   221→        if name in confirmed:
   222→            continue
   223→
   224→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   225→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   226→        blocked_by = sorted(
   227→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   228→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   229→        )
   230→
   231→        cmd = f"desloppify plan triage --stage {name}"
   232→        if name == "commit":
   233→            cmd = 'desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."'
   234→
   235→        items.append({
   236→            "id": sid,
   237→            "tier": 1,
   238→            "confidence": "high",
   239→            "detector": "triage",
   240→            "file": ".",
   241→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   242→            "stage_name": name,
   243→            "stage_index": idx,
   244→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",

   248→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
```

```
   348→) -> list[dict]:
   349→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   350→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   351→    if not dim_scores:
   352→        return []
```

```
1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions``, ``auto_cluster``, and ``_work_queue/core``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    13→
    14→# Detectors whose findings are NOT objective mechanical work.
    15→# Canonical definition — re-exported by stale_dimensions for back-compat.
    16→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    17→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    18→})
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    22→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    23→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    24→
    25→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any open non-subjective findings?
    26→    objective_count: int  # how many
    27→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    28→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    29→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    30→
    31→    def should_surface(self, item: dict) -> bool:
    32→        """Should this subjective queue item appear in the work queue?
    33→
    34→        Unscored (initial_review) -> always.  All others -> only when drained.
    35→        """
    36→        if item.get("initial_review"):
    37→            return True
    38→        return not self.has_objective_backlog
    39→
    40→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    41→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    42→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    43→            return True  # unconditional
    44→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    45→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    46→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    47→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    48→        return False
    49→
    50→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    51→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    52→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    53→            return False  # never evict unscored
    54→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    55→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    56→        return False
    57→
    58→    @property
    59→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    60→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    61→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    62→
    63→
    64→def _is_evidence_only(finding: dict) -> bool:
    65→    """Return True if the finding is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    66→    from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    67→    from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    68→
    69→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    70→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    71→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    72→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    73→        finding_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    74→        if finding_rank > threshold_rank:
    75→            return True
    76→    return False
    77→
    78→
    79→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = object()
    80→
```

```
80→
    81→
    82→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    83→    state: StateModel,
    84→    *,
    85→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
    86→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    87→    plan: dict | None = None,
    88→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    89→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    90→
    91→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    92→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    93→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, findings whose IDs
    94→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    95→
    96→    Imports building-block helpers from ``stale_dimensions`` so the
    97→    source-of-truth logic stays in one place.
    98→    """
    99→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   100→        _current_stale_ids,
   101→        current_under_target_ids,
   102→        current_unscored_ids,
   103→    )
   104→    from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import finding_in_scan_scope
   105→
   106→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
   107→        state.get("scan_path")
   108→        if scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY
   109→        else scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   110→    )
   111→
   112→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   113→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   114→
   115→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective findings.
   116→    # Evidence-only findings (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   117→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   118→    # Findings outside scan_path and plan-skipped findings are also excluded
   119→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   120→    objective_count = sum(
   121→        1
   122→        for fid, f in findings.items()
   123→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   124→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   125→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   126→        and not _is_evidence_only(f)
   127→        and finding_in_scan_scope(str(f.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   128→        and fid not in skipped_ids
   129→    )
   130→
   131→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   132→    stale = _current_stale_ids(state)
   133→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   134→
   135→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   136→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   137→        objective_count=objective_count,
   138→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   139→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   140→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   141→    )
   142→
   143→
   144→__all__ = [
   145→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   146→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   147→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   148→]
   149→
```

```
    51→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:

    54→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    55→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    56→            continue
    57→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    58→        if strict_val < threshold:
    59→            low.append(
    60→                (
    61→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    62→                    strict_val,
    63→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    64→                )
    65→            )
    66→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    67→    return low

    97→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    98→        if active_cluster:
    99→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   100→    return effective_cluster
```

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me compile a comprehensive call-site analysis report.

## Consolidated Queue Context Refactoring - Call Site Analysis

I've reviewed all eight call sites that will need to change in a QueueContext consolidation. Here's the complete inconsistency map:

### 1. **app/commands/next.py** (lines 182-211)
**Location:** `_get_items()` function, lines 182-210

**Pattern:** Full load_plan + build_work_queue with most options
```python
plan = load_plan()
plan_data: dict | None = None
if (
    plan.get("queue_order")
    or plan.get("overrides")
    or plan.get("clusters")
):
    plan_data = plan

queue = build_work_queue(
    state,
    options=QueueBuildOptions(
        count=count,
        scope=scope,
        status=status,
        include_subjective=True,
        subjective_threshold=target_strict,
        explain=explain,
        plan=plan_data,
        include_skipped=include_skipped,
        cluster=effective_cluster,
    ),
)
```

**Parameters passed:** count, scope, status, include_subjective, subjective_threshold, explain, plan, include_skipped, cluster

**Omitted:** policy (computed internally)

---

### 2. **app/commands/status_cmd.py** (lines 97-122)
**Location:** `cmd_status()` function, lines 97-122

**Pattern:** Loads plan separately, then calls `compute_subjective_visibility()` manually
```python
_plan = load_plan()
_plan_active = _plan if (
    _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
) else None

# ... later ...
from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_subjective_visibility
_policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
    state,
    target_strict=target_strict_score,
    plan=_plan_active,
)
_objective_backlog = _policy.objective_count
```

**Key difference:** Calls `compute_subjective_visibility()` directly with `target_strict` parameter, storing result in `_policy` variable, then uses `_policy.objective_count`

**Parameters used with plan:** target_strict, plan — but NOT passed to build_work_queue

---

### 3. **app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py** (lines 205-256)
**Location:** `_reconcile_plan_post_scan()` function, lines 205-256 (especially 221-230, 238-243, 249-252)

**Pattern:** Loads plan + config, computes target_strict, then calls `compute_subjective_visibility()` once and threads it through multiple sync operations
```python
plan = load_plan(plan_path)
_target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)

_policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
    runtime.state,
    target_strict=_target_strict,
    plan=plan,
)

_cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")

# ... then reused in multiple places ...
stale_changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
    plan, runtime.state,
    lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
        p, s, policy=_policy, cycle_just_completed=_cycle_just_completed,
    ),
)

auto_changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
    plan, runtime.state, target_strict=_target_strict, policy=_policy,
    cycle_just_completed=_cycle_just_completed,
)
```

**Key insight:** Policy is computed ONCE and reused across:
- `sync_stale_dimensions()`
- `auto_cluster_findings()`
- `sync_score_checkpoint_needed()`
- `sync_create_plan_needed()`

This pattern avoids recomputing policy multiple times in a single reconciliation pass.

---

### 4. **app/commands/review/preflight.py** (lines 85-96)
**Location:** `_objective_and_subjective_backlog()` function, lines 85-96

**Pattern:** Load plan, compute policy, extract objective_count
```python
def _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    blocking_dims: list[str],
) -> tuple[int, int]:
    plan_data = load_plan()
    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, plan=plan_data)
    objective_total = policy.objective_count
    return objective_total, 0
```

**Parameters passed:** plan (no target_strict!)

**Constraint:** No target_strict parameter passed to compute_subjective_visibility

---

### 5. **app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py** (lines 40-114)
**Location:** `plan_aware_queue_breakdown()` function, lines 40-114

**Pattern:** Minimal options — calls build_work_queue without subjective_threshold, explain, count, cluster, or status filter
```python
result = build_work_queue(
    state,
    options=QueueBuildOptions(
        status="open",
        count=None,
        plan=plan,
        include_skipped=False,
    ),
)
```

**Omitted:** subjective_threshold (defaults to 100.0), explain, cluster, include_subjective (defaults to True)

**Purpose:** Generic queue counting — wants all items with default settings

---

### 6. **app/commands/resolve/queue_guard.py** (lines 27-34)
**Location:** `_check_queue_order_guard()` function, lines 27-34

**Pattern:** Minimal options — builds queue only for front-item checking
```python
result = build_work_queue(
    state,
    options=QueueBuildOptions(
        plan=plan,
        count=None,
        include_subjective=True,
    ),
)
```

**Omitted:** status (defaults to "open"), subjective_threshold (defaults to 100.0), explain, cluster

**Purpose:** Check if resolved items are at the front of the queue

---

### 7. **app/commands/helpers/guardrails.py**
**Location:** Not using build_work_queue directly

This file defines `print_triage_guardrail_info()` which loads plan but does NOT call build_work_queue. It calls:
- `triage_guardrail_status()` (which loads plan)
- `is_triage_stale()`
- `compute_new_finding_ids()`
- `triage_phase_banner()`

**No change needed here** — no queue building.

---

### 8. **engine/_work_queue/core.py** (lines 76-221)
**Location:** `build_work_queue()` function definition, lines 76-221

**Key internal logic (lines 113-116):**
```python
policy = resolved_options.policy or compute_subjective_visibility(
    state,
    plan=resolved_options.plan,
)
```

**Current behavior:**
- If `policy` is passed in QueueBuildOptions, use it
- Otherwise, compute it internally from state + plan (with NO target_strict)
- Note: target_strict is NOT read from config; it's only extracted from options.subjective_threshold

**This is the core inconsistency:** compute_subjective_visibility is called without target_strict, but status_cmd.py and scan_workflow.py both pass target_strict when they compute policy externally.

---

## Summary of Inconsistencies

| Call Site | Loads Plan | Computes Policy | Passes target_strict to policy | Passes policy to build_work_queue | Reuses policy | 
|-----------|-----------|-----------------|-------------------------------|----------------------------------|---------------|
| next.py | Yes | No (internal) | No | No (implicit) | No |
| status_cmd.py | Yes | Yes, externally | Yes | No | Yes (for display) |
| scan_workflow.py | Yes | Yes, once | Yes | No (threaded manually) | Yes (multi-function) |
| preflight.py | Yes | Yes, externally | **No** | No | No |
| queue_progress.py | No | No (internal) | No | No (implicit) | No |
| queue_guard.py | Yes | No (internal) | No | No (implicit) | No |
| guardrails.py | Yes | No (uses different functions) | N/A | No | N/A |
| core.py (build_work_queue) | No | Yes, conditionally | **No** (only from subjective_threshold) | N/A (internal) | N/A |

---

## Key Insights for QueueContext Design

1. **target_strict threading issue:** status_cmd.py and scan_workflow.py both derive a target_strict score from config and pass it to `compute_subjective_visibility()`. But `build_work_queue()` receives target_strict only indirectly via `subjective_threshold` in options. A unified QueueContext should accept target_strict explicitly.

2. **Policy reuse pattern:** scan_workflow.py and status_cmd.py both compute policy once and reuse it across multiple operations. next.py and queue_progress.py don't need explicit policy handling because they use default settings. QueueContext should support both patterns: optional pre-computed policy OR lazy internal computation.

3. **Plan activation logic varies:** next.py and status_cmd.py both check `if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")` (with status_cmd also checking "overrides" implicitly). scan_workflow.py uses `_plan_has_user_content()` which also checks "skipped" and "overrides". Queue loading should be standardized.

4. **Minimal consumers:** queue_progress.py and queue_guard.py call build_work_queue with nearly default options. These would benefit from a simpler API or factory function.

5. **scan_workflow.py is the highest-value consolidation target:** It currently computes policy once and passes it to 4 different functions (sync_stale_dimensions, sync_score_checkpoint_needed, sync_create_plan_needed, auto_cluster_findings). Moving this into a QueueContext would eliminate parameter threading.

---

```
1→"""Language runtime-option parsing helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→class LangRuntimeOptionsError(ValueError):
    11→    """Raised when --lang-opt values cannot be parsed or normalized."""
    12→
    13→    def __init__(self, message: str, *, supported_options: list[str] | None = None):
    14→        super().__init__(message)
    15→        self.supported_options = supported_options or []
    16→
    17→
    18→def parse_lang_opt_assignments(raw_values: list[str] | None) -> dict[str, str]:
    19→    """Parse repeated KEY=VALUE --lang-opt inputs."""
    20→    values = raw_values or []
    21→    parsed: dict[str, str] = {}
    22→    for raw in values:
    23→        text = (raw or "").strip()
    24→        if not text:
    25→            continue
    26→        if "=" not in text:
    27→            raise ValueError(f"Invalid --lang-opt '{raw}'. Expected KEY=VALUE.")
    28→        key, value = text.split("=", 1)
    29→        key = key.strip().replace("-", "_")
    30→        if not key:
    31→            raise ValueError(f"Invalid --lang-opt '{raw}'. Missing option key.")
    32→        parsed[key] = value.strip()
    33→    return parsed
    34→
    35→
    36→def resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang) -> dict[str, object]:
    37→    """Resolve runtime options from generic --lang-opt inputs."""
    38→    try:
    39→        options = parse_lang_opt_assignments(getattr(args, "lang_opt", None))
    40→    except ValueError as exc:
    41→        raise LangRuntimeOptionsError(str(exc)) from exc
    42→
    43→    try:
    44→        return lang.normalize_runtime_options(options, strict=True)
    45→    except KeyError as exc:
    46→        supported = sorted((lang.runtime_option_specs or {}).keys())
    47→        raise LangRuntimeOptionsError(
    48→            str(exc),
    49→            supported_options=supported,
    50→        ) from exc
    51→
    52→
    53→def print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc: LangRuntimeOptionsError, *, lang_name: str) -> None:
    54→    """Render a runtime-option parse/validation error."""
    55→    print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
    56→    hint = ", ".join(exc.supported_options) if exc.supported_options else "(none)"
    57→    print(
    58→        colorize(f"  Supported {lang_name} runtime options: {hint}", "dim"),
    59→        file=sys.stderr,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = [
    64→    "LangRuntimeOptionsError",
    65→    "parse_lang_opt_assignments",
    66→    "print_lang_runtime_options_error",
    67→    "resolve_lang_runtime_options",
    68→]
    69→
```

## Summary: QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue, and Related Patterns

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's the complete picture:

---

### 1. QueueBuildOptions Dataclass Definition

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` lines 44-65

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueBuildOptions:
    """Configuration for queue construction.

    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    """

    count: int | None = 1
    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    scope: str | None = None
    status: str = "open"
    include_subjective: bool = True
    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    chronic: bool = False
    explain: bool = False
    plan: dict | None = None
    include_skipped: bool = False
    cluster: str | None = None
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None
```

**Key field notes:**
- `count`: Limits returned items (default 1), None = unlimited
- `scan_path`: Sentinel value `_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE` auto-reads from state["scan_path"]
- `scope`: Pattern matching for filtering (file paths, detector names, dimension names, or glob patterns)
- `status`: Filter type ("open", "resolved", "all") — validated against `ALL_STATUSES`
- `include_subjective`: Whether to build synthetic subjective dimension items
- `subjective_threshold`: **Float [0-100]** — dimensions with strict scores >= this are excluded from queue (lines 95-98 clamp to 0-100)
- `chronic`: If True, only shows findings with reopen_count >= 2
- `explain`: Adds "explain" key to each item
- `plan`: Dict containing plan queue_order, skipped, clusters, etc. for plan-aware ordering
- `include_skipped`: Whether to include plan-skipped items
- `cluster`: Focus on specific cluster ID
- `policy`: Pre-computed SubjectiveVisibility (computed fresh if None)

---

### 2. build_work_queue Function — Signature and First ~50 Lines

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` lines 76-222

```python
def build_work_queue(
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
) -> WorkQueueResult:
    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()

    # Resolve scan_path: sentinel → read from state, explicit value → use as-is
    scan_path: str | None = (
        state.get("scan_path")
        if resolved_options.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
        else resolved_options.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
    )

    status = resolved_options.status
    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    try:
        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))

    finding_items = build_finding_items(
        state,
        scan_path=scan_path,
        status_filter=status,
        scope=resolved_options.scope,
        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
    )

    all_items = list(finding_items)

    # Resolve subjective visibility policy for gating.
    # compute_subjective_visibility auto-reads scan_path from state, matching
    # the scan_path we resolved above (both read state["scan_path"]).
    policy = resolved_options.policy or compute_subjective_visibility(
        state,
        plan=resolved_options.plan,
    )

    if (
        resolved_options.include_subjective
        and status in {"open", "all"}
        and not resolved_options.chronic
    ):
        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
            state,
            state.get("findings", {}),
            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
        )
        # When a plan explicitly includes a subjective item in queue_order,
        # surface it regardless of policy — the plan is authoritative.
```

---

### 3. How subjective_threshold is Used

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` lines 95-143

1. **Parsing & Normalization** (lines 95-98):
   - Coerced to float; on parse error defaults to 100.0
   - Clamped to [0.0, 100.0]

2. **Passed to build_subjective_items** (line 126):
   ```python
   subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
       state,
       state.get("findings", {}),
       threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   )
   ```

3. **Inside build_subjective_items** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` lines 346-441):
   - Normalized again: `threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))`
   - **Used as filtering gate** (line 393):
     ```python
     strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
     if strict_val >= threshold:
         continue  # SKIP dimensions at or above threshold
     ```
   - **Semantic:** Subjective dimensions with strict scores *below* the threshold are surfaced; dimensions *at or above* the threshold are skipped

4. **Gating by policy** (lines 136-143):
   - Non-initial subjective items only surface if:
     - `policy.should_surface(item)` returns True (i.e., objective backlog is drained), OR
     - The item ID appears explicitly in `plan.queue_order` (plan is authoritative)

---

### 4. plan_aware_queue_breakdown and QueueBreakdown

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py` lines 16-114

**QueueBreakdown dataclass** (lines 16-38):
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueBreakdown:
    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""

    queue_total: int = 0
    plan_ordered: int = 0
    skipped: int = 0
    subjective: int = 0
    workflow: int = 0
    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    focus_cluster_total: int = 0

    @property
    def actionable(self) -> int:
        """Items that represent real work — excludes workflow navigation steps."""
        return max(0, self.queue_total - self.workflow)
```

**plan_aware_queue_breakdown function** (lines 40-114):
- Single call to `build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(...))`
- Collapses clusters if plan exists and no active_cluster is set
- Counts subjective items: `is_subjective_queue_item(item)` checks kind == "subjective_dimension" or cluster with all subjective members
- Counts workflow items: kind in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
- Plan-derived counts:
  - `skipped`: len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
  - `plan_ordered`: items in queue_order minus skipped
- Focus cluster counts cluster members in the open state

**Key property:**
- `actionable`: queue_total - workflow (excludes navigation steps like "run-scan")

---

### 5. target_strict_score_from_config Function

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/score.py` lines 31-40

```python
def target_strict_score_from_config(
    config: dict | None, *, fallback: float = 95.0
) -> float:
    """Read and normalize target strict score from config."""
    if isinstance(config, dict):
        raw = config.get("target_strict_score", fallback)
    else:
        raw = fallback
    return coerce_target_score(raw, fallback=fallback)
```

**What it does:**
1. Reads `config["target_strict_score"]` (raw value can be numeric, string, or invalid)
2. Delegates to `coerce_target_score()` (lines 8-28):
   - Normalizes any value (numeric, string, or invalid) to float
   - Falls back to 95.0 on parse failure
   - Clamps to [0.0, 100.0]
   - Returns the normalized float

**Usage example** (from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next.py` line 205):
```python
target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
# ...
options=QueueBuildOptions(
    # ...
    subjective_threshold=target_strict,
    # ...
)
```

### 6. Existing Runtime Context Patterns

The codebase already establishes two complementary patterns for runtime context:

**A. CommandRuntime dataclass** — for command handlers
**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/runtime.py` lines 14-36

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CommandRuntime:
    """Explicit runtime dependencies shared by command handlers."""

    config: dict[str, Any]
    state: dict[str, Any]
    state_path: Path | None


def command_runtime(args) -> CommandRuntime:
    """Return runtime context from explicit args.runtime or construct one."""
    runtime = getattr(args, "runtime", None)
    if isinstance(runtime, CommandRuntime):
        return runtime

    config = load_config()
    state_file = state_path(args)
    if isinstance(state_file, str):
        state_file = Path(state_file)

    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)

    return CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
```

**Key pattern:**
- Frozen dataclass (immutable, safe to pass around)
- Can be pre-constructed and attached to args.runtime
- Factory function `command_runtime(args)` returns existing or creates new
- Single point of config + state loading

**B. ScanRuntime dataclass** — for scan orchestration
**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py`

```python
@dataclass  # (inferred from scan_orchestrator usage)
class ScanRuntime:
    """Resolved runtime context for a single scan invocation."""

    args: argparse.Namespace
    state_path: Path | None
    state: state_mod.StateModel
    path: Path
    config: dict[str, object]
    lang: LangRun | None
    lang_label: str
    profile: str
    effective_include_slow: bool
    zone_overrides: dict[str, object] | None
    reset_subjective_count: int = 0
    expired_manual_override_count: int = 0
    coverage_warnings: list[DetectorCoverageRecord] = field(default_factory=list)
```

**Key pattern:**
- Richer than CommandRuntime — includes lang, path, profile, and scan-specific counters
- Encapsulates all state needed by `ScanOrchestrator` methods
- Methods receive the whole runtime, not individual args

**C. RuntimeContext** — lower-level process/thread runtime state
**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/core/runtime_state.py` lines 58-102

```python
@dataclass
class RuntimeContext:
    """Mutable runtime container for exclusion and cache state."""

    exclusions: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    project_root: Path | None = None
    query_file: Path | None = None
    file_text_cache: FileTextCache = field(default_factory=FileTextCache)
    cache_enabled: bool = False
    source_file_cache: SourceFileCache = field(
        default_factory=lambda: SourceFileCache(max_entries=16)
    )


_PROCESS_RUNTIME_CONTEXT = RuntimeContext()
_RUNTIME_CONTEXT: ContextVar[RuntimeContext | None] = ContextVar(
    "desloppify_runtime_context",
    default=None,
)


def current_runtime_context() -> RuntimeContext:
    """Return the active runtime context (or process fallback)."""
    runtime = _RUNTIME_CONTEXT.get()
    if runtime is not None:
        return runtime
    return _PROCESS_RUNTIME_CONTEXT


@contextmanager
def runtime_scope(runtime: RuntimeContext | None = None):
    """Run code with an isolated runtime context."""
    active = runtime or make_runtime_context()
    [REDACTED](active)
    try:
        yield active
    finally:
        _RUNTIME_CONTEXT.reset(token)
```

**Key pattern:**
- Uses `contextvars.ContextVar` for async-safe thread-local storage
- Mutable (unlike CommandRuntime), optimized for caches
- Defaults to process-level singleton for backward compatibility
- `runtime_scope()` context manager for isolated scopes
