### Summary for Your Refactoring

**QueueBuildContext Pattern** (if you create one):

Based on existing patterns, a new context dataclass would:
1. Be **frozen** (immutable, like CommandRuntime) for safety
2. Contain the resolved option values (not the raw QueueBuildOptions)
3. Include pre-computed policy and other derived state
4. Allow optional pre-binding via `args.queue_context` (following CommandRuntime)
5. Possibly include a factory function like `queue_build_context(state, options, config)` to handle resolution logic

**Example skeleton:**
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueBuildContext:
    """Resolved runtime context for work-queue construction."""
    
    state: StateModel
    config: dict[str, Any]
    scan_path: str | None
    subjective_threshold: float
    status: str
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility
    plan: dict | None
    # ... other resolved values
```

This keeps `build_work_queue()` clean while centralizing threshold resolution, policy computation, and config-driven defaults in one place.

> DEVELOPER

Design an implementation plan for refactoring the work queue counting system in this codebase. The goal: eliminate the class of bugs where different code paths compute "how much work remains?" differently because they thread parameters inconsistently.

## Current Problem

Multiple call sites construct queue counts with different parameter combinations, leading to bugs like:
- Missing `plan` parameter → skipped findings counted as backlog (#197)
- Missing `target_strict` → wrong subjective dimension thresholds
- Circular subjective blocking in preflight (#198)
- Workflow items counted inconsistently

## Current Architecture

### QueueBuildOptions (engine/_work_queue/core.py)
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueBuildOptions:
    count: int | None = 1
    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    scope: str | None = None
    status: str = "open"
    include_subjective: bool = True
    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    chronic: bool = False
    explain: bool = False
    plan: dict | None = None
    include_skipped: bool = False
    cluster: str | None = None
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None
```

### build_work_queue (engine/_work_queue/core.py)
- Takes state + QueueBuildOptions
- Resolves scan_path from state if sentinel
- Computes policy internally if not provided (WITHOUT target_strict)
- Returns WorkQueueResult dict

### Inconsistency Map (current callers)

| Caller | plan | scan_path | target_strict to policy | policy to build_wq |
|--------|------|-----------|------------------------|-------------------|
| next.py | ✓ | ✓ | ✗ (via subjective_threshold only) | ✗ |
| status_cmd.py | ✓ | auto | ✓ | ✗ (not calling build_wq) |
| scan_workflow.py | ✓ | auto | ✓ | ✗ |
| preflight.py | ✓ | auto | ✗ | ✗ |
| queue_progress.py | via caller | auto | ✗ | ✗ |
| queue_guard.py | ✓ | auto | ✗ | ✗ |
| build_work_queue internal | from options | from state | ✗ | N/A |

### Existing patterns to follow
- `CommandRuntime` in app/commands/helpers/runtime.py — frozen dataclass + factory function
- `ScanRuntime` in scan_workflow.py — richer frozen context for orchestration
- `RuntimeContext` in core/runtime_state.py — contextvars-based mutable context

### Key functions
- `compute_subjective_visibility(state, *, target_strict=95.0, scan_path=None, plan=None)` in engine/_plan/subjective_policy.py
- `target_strict_score_from_config(config, *, fallback=95.0)` in app/commands/helpers/score.py
- `load_plan()` in engine/_plan/persistence.py — returns empty plan if missing
- `plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)` in app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py
- `build_work_queue(state, *, options)` in engine/_work_queue/core.py

## Design Constraints

1. **Minimize blast radius** — this should be a targeted refactoring, not a rewrite
2. **Follow existing patterns** — CommandRuntime is the closest precedent
3. **Make the wrong thing hard** — the new API should make it impossible to forget parameters
4. **Backward compatibility** — QueueBuildOptions still works, QueueContext is opt-in initially
5. **No behavioral changes** — same outputs, just consolidated parameter passing
6. **Keep scan_workflow.py's pattern** of computing policy once and reusing it across multiple operations

## What I want from you

Design a concrete, file-by-file implementation plan. Specifically:

1. Where does QueueContext live? (engine layer? app layer?)
2. What fields does it have?
3. What's the factory function signature?
4. How does it integrate with QueueBuildOptions (replacement? wrapper? passed into it?)
5. What changes at each call site?
6. What about plan_aware_queue_breakdown — does it take QueueContext?
7. How does preflight simplify?
8. What tests need updating?
9. What order should the changes happen in?

Think about the minimum viable refactoring that fixes the parameter-threading problem without over-engineering.

```
    44→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    45→class QueueBuildOptions:
    46→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    47→
    48→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    49→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    50→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    51→    """
    52→
    53→    count: int | None = 1
    54→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    55→    scope: str | None = None
    56→    status: str = "open"

    62→    include_skipped: bool = False

    66→
    67→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    68→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    69→
    70→    items: list[dict]
    71→    total: int
    72→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]

    75→
    76→def build_work_queue(
    77→    state: StateModel,
    78→    *,
    79→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    80→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    81→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    82→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    83→
    84→    # Resolve scan_path: sentinel → read from state, explicit value → use as-is
    85→    scan_path: str | None = (
    86→        state.get("scan_path")
    87→        if resolved_options.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    88→        else resolved_options.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
    89→    )
    90→
    91→    status = resolved_options.status
    92→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    93→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    94→    try:
    95→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    96→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    97→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    98→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    99→
   100→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   101→        state,
   102→        scan_path=scan_path,
   103→        status_filter=status,
   104→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   105→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   109→
   110→    # Resolve subjective visibility policy for gating.
   111→    # compute_subjective_visibility auto-reads scan_path from state, matching
   112→    # the scan_path we resolved above (both read state["scan_path"]).
   113→    policy = resolved_options.policy or compute_subjective_visibility(
   114→        state,
   115→        plan=resolved_options.plan,
   116→    )
   117→
   118→    if (
   119→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   120→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   121→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   122→    ):
   123→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   124→            state,
   125→            state.get("findings", {}),
   126→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   127→        )
   128→        # When a plan explicitly includes a subjective item in queue_order,
   129→        # surface it regardless of policy — the plan is authoritative.
   130→        plan_queue_set: set[str] = (
   131→            set(resolved_options.plan.get("queue_order", []))
   132→            if resolved_options.plan
   133→            else set()
   134→        )
   135→        for item in subjective_items:
   136→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   137→                continue
   138→            # Non-initial subjective items only surface when objective
   139→            # backlog is drained — unless the plan explicitly queued them
   140→            # (e.g. stale dims injected after a completed cycle).
   141→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   142→            if not policy.should_surface(item) and item_id not in plan_queue_set:
   143→                continue
   144→            all_items.append(item)
   145→
   146→    # Inject triage stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   147→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   148→        synth_items = build_triage_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   149→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   150→        checkpoint_item = build_score_checkpoint_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   151→        if checkpoint_item is not None:
   152→            all_items.append(checkpoint_item)
   153→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   154→        if plan_item is not None:
   155→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   156→
   157→    enrich_with_impact(all_items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   158→
   159→    # Impact floor: drop mechanical findings with negligible score impact
   160→    MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   161→    all_items = [
   162→        item for item in all_items
   163→        if item.get("kind") != "finding"
   164→        or item.get("is_review")
   165→        or item.get("is_subjective")
   166→        or not item.get("estimated_impact")
   167→        or item["estimated_impact"] >= MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   168→    ]
   169→
   170→    # Plan-aware pipeline: enrich, stamp sort keys, separate skipped,
   171→    # then a single sort produces the correct ordering.
   172→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   173→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   174→    if resolved_options.plan:
   175→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   176→        _enrich_plan_metadata(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   177→        _stamp_plan_sort_keys(all_items, resolved_options.plan, new_ids)
   178→        all_items, skipped_items = _separate_skipped(
   179→            all_items, resolved_options.plan,
   180→        )
   181→
   182→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   183→
   184→    if resolved_options.plan:
   185→        if resolved_options.include_skipped:
   186→            all_items = all_items + skipped_items
   187→        _stamp_positions(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   188→        all_items = _filter_cluster_focus(
   189→            all_items, resolved_options.plan, resolved_options.cluster,
   190→        )
   191→
   192→    # Inject "run scan" workflow item when queue is empty and a plan cycle
   193→    # is active (plan_start_scores exist).  This gives the user an explicit
   194→    # next-step rather than a bare "nothing to do" message.
   195→    if not all_items and resolved_options.plan:
   196→        plan_scores = resolved_options.plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   197→        if plan_scores.get("strict") is not None:
   198→            all_items.append({
   199→                "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   200→                "kind": "workflow_action",
   201→                "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   202→                "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   203→                "file": "",
   204→                "detector": "workflow",
   205→                "confidence": "high",
   206→            })
   207→
   208→    total = len(all_items)
   209→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   210→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   211→
   212→    if resolved_options.explain:
   213→        for item in all_items:
   214→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   215→
   216→    return {
   217→        "items": all_items,
   218→        "total": total,

   220→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   221→    }
```

```
1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions``, ``auto_cluster``, and ``_work_queue/core``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    13→
    14→# Detectors whose findings are NOT objective mechanical work.
    15→# Canonical definition — re-exported by stale_dimensions for back-compat.
    16→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    17→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    18→})
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    22→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    23→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    24→
    25→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any open non-subjective findings?
    26→    objective_count: int  # how many
    27→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    28→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    29→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    30→
    31→    def should_surface(self, item: dict) -> bool:
    32→        """Should this subjective queue item appear in the work queue?
    33→
    34→        Unscored (initial_review) -> always.  All others -> only when drained.
    35→        """
    36→        if item.get("initial_review"):
    37→            return True
    38→        return not self.has_objective_backlog
    39→
    40→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    41→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    42→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    43→            return True  # unconditional
    44→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    45→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    46→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    47→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    48→        return False
    49→
    50→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    51→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    52→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    53→            return False  # never evict unscored
    54→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    55→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    56→        return False
    57→
    58→    @property
    59→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    60→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    61→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    62→
    63→
    64→def _is_evidence_only(finding: dict) -> bool:
    65→    """Return True if the finding is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    66→    from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    67→    from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    68→
    69→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    70→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    71→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    72→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    73→        finding_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    74→        if finding_rank > threshold_rank:
    75→            return True
    76→    return False
    77→
    78→
    79→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = object()
    80→
    81→
    82→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    83→    state: StateModel,
    84→    *,
    85→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
    86→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    87→    plan: dict | None = None,
    88→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    89→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    90→
    91→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    92→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    93→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, findings whose IDs
    94→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    95→
    96→    Imports building-block helpers from ``stale_dimensions`` so the
    97→    source-of-truth logic stays in one place.
    98→    """
    99→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   100→        _current_stale_ids,
   101→        current_under_target_ids,
   102→        current_unscored_ids,
   103→    )
   104→    from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import finding_in_scan_scope
   105→
   106→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
   107→        state.get("scan_path")
   108→        if scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY
   109→        else scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   110→    )
   111→
   112→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   113→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   114→
   115→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective findings.
   116→    # Evidence-only findings (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   117→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   118→    # Findings outside scan_path and plan-skipped findings are also excluded
   119→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   120→    objective_count = sum(
   121→        1
   122→        for fid, f in findings.items()
   123→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   124→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   125→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   126→        and not _is_evidence_only(f)
   127→        and finding_in_scan_scope(str(f.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   128→        and fid not in skipped_ids
   129→    )
   130→
   131→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   132→    stale = _current_stale_ids(state)
   133→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   134→
   135→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   136→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   137→        objective_count=objective_count,
   138→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   139→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   140→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   141→    )
   142→
   143→
   144→__all__ = [
   145→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   146→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   147→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   148→]
   149→
```

```
1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import is_subjective_queue_item
    10→
    11→
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→
    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class QueueBreakdown:
    18→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    19→
    20→    queue_total: int = 0
    21→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    22→    skipped: int = 0
    23→    subjective: int = 0
    24→    workflow: int = 0
    25→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    26→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    27→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    28→
    29→    @property
    30→    def actionable(self) -> int:
    31→        """Items that represent real work — excludes workflow navigation steps.
    32→
    33→        Use this instead of ``queue_total`` when gating on "is there work left?"
    34→        (e.g. scan preflight, plan-start-score clearing).  Workflow items like
    35→        ``workflow::run-scan`` guide the user but are not work to complete.
    36→        """
    37→        return max(0, self.queue_total - self.workflow)
    38→
    39→
    40→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    41→    state: dict,
    42→    plan: dict | None = None,
    43→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    44→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call."""
    45→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    46→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    47→
    48→    result = build_work_queue(
    49→        state,
    50→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    51→            status="open",
    52→            count=None,
    53→            plan=plan,
    54→            include_skipped=False,
    55→        ),
    56→    )
    57→
    58→    # Collapse clusters for display-level counting
    59→    items = result.get("items", [])
    60→    if plan and not plan.get("active_cluster"):
    61→        items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
    62→
    63→    queue_total = len(items)
    64→
    65→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
    66→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
    67→    subjective = sum(
    68→        1 for item in items
    69→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    70→    )
    71→    workflow = sum(
    72→        1 for item in items
    73→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    74→    )
    75→
    76→    # Plan-derived counts
    77→    plan_ordered = 0
    78→    skipped = 0
    79→    if plan:
    80→        skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    81→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
    82→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    83→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    84→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    85→
    86→    # Focus cluster info
    87→    focus_cluster = None
    88→    focus_cluster_count = 0
    89→    focus_cluster_total = 0
    90→    if plan:
    91→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    92→        if active:
    93→            focus_cluster = active
    94→            cluster_data = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    95→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    96→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
    97→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            open_findings = {
    99→                fid
   100→                for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   101→                if f.get("status") == "open"
   102→            }
   103→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_findings)
   104→
   105→    return QueueBreakdown(
   106→        queue_total=queue_total,
   107→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
   108→        skipped=skipped,
   109→        subjective=subjective,
   110→        workflow=workflow,
   111→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
   112→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
   113→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   114→    )
   115→
   116→
   117→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
   119→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   120→
   121→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   122→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   123→
   124→    Examples::
   125→
   126→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   127→        Queue: 1934 items
   128→    """
   129→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   130→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   131→
   132→    # Parenthesized segments
   133→    segments: list[str] = []
   134→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   135→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.workflow} planning step{'s' if breakdown.workflow != 1 else ''}")
   136→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   137→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   138→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   139→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   140→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   141→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   142→    if segments:
   143→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   144→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   145→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   146→    return label
   147→
   148→
   149→def format_queue_block(
   150→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   151→    *,
   152→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   153→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   154→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   155→
   156→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   157→    """
   158→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   159→
   160→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   161→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   162→        focus_line = (
   163→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   164→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   165→            f" items remaining"
   166→        )
   167→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   168→
   169→    # Frozen score line
   170→    if frozen_score is not None:
   171→        lines.append((
   172→            f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   173→            "cyan",
   174→        ))
   175→
   176→    # Queue headline — always the same
   177→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   178→
   179→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   180→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   181→        lines.append((
   182→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   183→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   184→            "dim",
   185→        ))
   186→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   187→        lines.append((
   188→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   189→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   190→            "dim",
   191→        ))
   192→    else:
   193→        lines.append((
   194→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   195→            "dim",
   196→        ))
   197→
   198→    return lines
   199→
   200→
   201→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   202→# Legacy / backward-compatible helpers
   203→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   204→
   205→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
   206→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed).
   207→
   208→    .. deprecated::
   209→        Returns ``queue_total`` which **includes** workflow navigation items.
   210→        Prefer ``plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan).actionable`` for
   211→        gate/preflight checks where workflow items should not count.
   212→    """
   213→    return plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan).queue_total
   214→
   215→
   216→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   217→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   218→    if not plan:
   219→        return None
   220→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   221→
   222→
   223→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   224→    plan: dict,
   225→    queue_remaining: int,
   226→    *,
   227→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None,
   228→) -> None:
   229→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   230→
   231→    When *breakdown* is provided, uses the standardized queue block.
   232→    Otherwise falls back to the legacy two-line format.
   233→    """
   234→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   235→    strict = scores.get("strict")
   236→    if strict is None:
   237→        return
   238→
   239→    if breakdown is not None:
   240→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=strict)
   241→        print()
   242→        for text, style in block:
   243→            print(colorize(text, style))
   244→        if queue_remaining > 0:
   245→            print(colorize(
   246→                "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   247→                "dim",
   248→            ))
   249→        return
   250→
   251→    # Legacy fallback
   252→    print(
   253→        colorize(
   254→            f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {strict:.1f}/100",
   255→            "cyan",
   256→        )
   257→    )
   258→    print(
   259→        colorize(
   260→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
   261→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   262→            "dim",
   263→        )
   264→    )
   265→
   266→
   267→def _print_objective_drained_banner(
   268→    frozen_strict: float,
   269→    remaining: int,
   270→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   271→) -> None:
   272→    """Show a phase-transition banner when objective work is drained."""
   273→    kind_labels: list[str] = []
   274→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   275→        kind_labels.append("subjective")
   276→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   277→        kind_labels.append("workflow")
   278→    kind_desc = " + ".join(kind_labels) if kind_labels else "non-objective"
   279→    print(colorize(
   280→        f"\n  Objective queue complete (plan-start was {frozen_strict:.1f})."
   281→        f" {remaining} {kind_desc} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''} remain.",
   282→        "cyan",
   283→    ))
   284→    print(colorize(
   285→        "  Run `desloppify next` for remaining work,"
   286→        " then `desloppify scan` to finalize.",
   287→        "dim",
   288→    ))
   289→
   290→
   291→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   292→    state: dict,
   293→    prev,
   294→    plan: dict | None,
   295→) -> None:
   296→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
   297→
   298→    Three display modes:
   299→
   300→    1. **Frozen** — objective queue items remain → show frozen plan-start score.
   301→    2. **Phase transition** — objective drained, subjective/workflow remains
   302→       → show live scores + transition banner.
   303→    3. **Live** — no active plan cycle or queue is clear → show live scores.
   304→    """
   305→    frozen_strict: float | None = None
   306→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None
   307→
   308→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
   309→        frozen_strict = plan["plan_start_scores"]["strict"]
   310→        try:
   311→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   312→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   313→            pass
   314→
   315→    remaining = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   316→
   317→    # Frozen: objective work remains — show frozen plan-start score and return
   318→    if remaining > 0 and frozen_strict is not None:
   319→        objective_remaining = remaining - breakdown.subjective - breakdown.workflow
   320→        if objective_remaining > 0:
   321→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   322→                plan, remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   323→            )
   324→            return
   325→
   326→    # Live scores (or phase transition): show current scores
   327→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
   328→
   329→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   330→
   331→    # Phase transition: objective drained but subjective/workflow remains
   332→    if remaining > 0 and frozen_strict is not None:
   333→        _print_objective_drained_banner(frozen_strict, remaining, breakdown)
   334→
   335→
   336→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   337→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   338→
   339→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   340→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   341→    """
   342→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   343→
   344→    try:
   345→        plan = load_plan()
   346→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   347→        plan = None
   348→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   349→
   350→
   351→__all__ = [
   352→    "QueueBreakdown",
   353→    "format_queue_block",
   354→    "format_queue_headline",
   355→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   356→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   357→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
   358→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   359→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   360→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   361→]
   362→
```

```
1→"""Preflight guards for subjective review reruns.
     2→
     3→Blocks reruns while backlog remains (objective findings and/or subjective queue
     4→work), unless ``--force-review-rerun`` is set. Also clears stale subjective
     5→markers before a new cycle.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import re
    11→import sys
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import CommandError
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_subjective_visibility, load_plan
    16→from desloppify.state import StateModel, save_state
    17→
    18→from .helpers import parse_dimensions
    19→
    20→
    21→def clear_stale_subjective_entries(
    22→    state: StateModel,
    23→    *,
    24→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    25→) -> list[str]:
    26→    """Clear ``needs_review_refresh`` markers from subjective assessments.
    27→
    28→    When *dimensions* is provided, only those dimensions are cleared;
    29→    otherwise all stale dimensions are cleared.
    30→
    31→    Returns the list of dimension keys that were cleared.
    32→    """
    33→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    34→    cleared: list[str] = []
    35→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    36→        if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
    37→            continue
    38→        if dimensions is not None and dim_key not in dimensions:
    39→            continue
    40→        if assessment.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    41→            assessment.pop("needs_review_refresh", None)
    42→            assessment.pop("stale_since", None)
    43→            assessment.pop("refresh_reason", None)
    44→            cleared.append(dim_key)
    45→    return cleared
    46→
    47→
    48→def _scored_dimensions(state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    49→    """Return dimension keys that already have a nonzero subjective score."""
    50→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    51→    scored: list[str] = []
    52→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    53→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
    54→            if assessment.get("score", 0):
    55→                scored.append(dim_key)
    56→        elif isinstance(assessment, (int, float)) and assessment:
    57→            scored.append(dim_key)
    58→    return sorted(scored)
    59→
    60→
    61→def _normalize_dimension_key(raw: object) -> str:
    62→    """Normalize a dimension key/name to canonical snake_case."""
    63→    text = str(raw or "").strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    64→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text).strip("_")
    65→
    66→
    67→
    68→def _blocking_scored_dimensions(
    69→    state: StateModel,
    70→    *,
    71→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
    72→    normalized_dimensions: set[str] | None,
    73→) -> list[str]:
    74→    scored_dims = _scored_dimensions(state)
    75→    if dimensions is None:
    76→        return scored_dims
    77→    normalized = normalized_dimensions or set()
    78→    return sorted(
    79→        dim
    80→        for dim in scored_dims
    81→        if _normalize_dimension_key(dim) in normalized
    82→    )
    83→
    84→
    85→def _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
    86→    state: StateModel,
    87→    *,
    88→    blocking_dims: list[str],
    89→) -> tuple[int, int]:
    90→    plan_data = load_plan()
    91→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, plan=plan_data)
    92→    objective_total = policy.objective_count
    93→    # Subjective dimensions are resolved BY running reviews, so they never
    94→    # block review --prepare (that would be circular).  Only objective
    95→    # findings constitute genuine blocking backlog.
    96→    return objective_total, 0
    97→
    98→
    99→def _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   100→    *,
   101→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   102→    objective_total: int,
   103→    subjective_total: int,
   104→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   105→) -> None:
   106→    print(
   107→        colorize(
   108→            "  Blocked: rerun requires drained backlog "
   109→            f"(objective: {objective_total}, subjective: {subjective_total}).",
   110→            "red",
   111→        ),
   112→        file=sys.stderr,
   113→    )
   114→    print(
   115→        colorize(
   116→            f"  Scored dimensions: {', '.join(blocking_dims)}",
   117→            "yellow",
   118→        ),
   119→        file=sys.stderr,
   120→    )
   121→    if objective_total > 0:
   122→        print(
   123→            colorize(
   124→                f"  Open objective finding(s): {objective_total}",
   125→                "yellow",
   126→            ),
   127→            file=sys.stderr,
   128→        )
   129→    if subjective_total > 0:
   130→        print(
   131→            colorize(
   132→                f"  Open subjective queue item(s): {subjective_total}",
   133→                "yellow",
   134→            ),
   135→            file=sys.stderr,
   136→        )
   137→    print("", file=sys.stderr)
   138→    if dimensions is not None:
   139→        unscored = sorted(dimensions - set(blocking_dims))
   140→        if unscored:
   141→            print(
   142→                colorize(
   143→                    f"  Tip: target only unscored dimensions with "
   144→                    f"--dimensions {','.join(unscored)}",
   145→                    "dim",
   146→                ),
   147→                file=sys.stderr,
   148→            )
   149→    print(
   150→        colorize(
   151→            "  Resolve open items first, or override with --force-review-rerun",
   152→            "dim",
   153→        ),
   154→        file=sys.stderr,
   155→    )
   156→
   157→
   158→def review_rerun_preflight(
   159→    state: StateModel,
   160→    args,
   161→    *,
   162→    state_file=None,
   163→    save_fn=save_state,
   164→) -> None:
   165→    """Single entry point: gate check -> clear stale -> save.
   166→
   167→    Exits with code 1 when open backlog exists and ``--force-review-rerun`` is
   168→    not set. On success, clears stale subjective markers for targeted
   169→    dimensions and persists state.
   170→    """
   171→    dimensions = parse_dimensions(args)
   172→    normalized_dimensions = (
   173→        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
   174→        if dimensions is not None
   175→        else None
   176→    )
   177→
   178→    # --force-review-rerun bypasses the gate
   179→    if getattr(args, "force_review_rerun", False):
   180→        print(
   181→            colorize(
   182→                "  --force-review-rerun: bypassing rerun backlog checks.",
   183→                "yellow",
   184→            )
   185→        )
   186→    else:
   187→        # Only gate dimensions that are actually targeted by this review run.
   188→        blocking_dims = _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   189→            state,
   190→            dimensions=dimensions,
   191→            normalized_dimensions=normalized_dimensions,
   192→        )
   193→
   194→        # No gate when none of the targeted dimensions have prior scores —
   195→        # this is a first run for these dimensions, not a rerun.
   196→        if blocking_dims:
   197→            objective_total, subjective_total = _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   198→                state,
   199→                blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   200→            )
   201→            if objective_total > 0 or subjective_total > 0:
   202→                _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   203→                    blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   204→                    objective_total=objective_total,
   205→                    subjective_total=subjective_total,
   206→                    dimensions=dimensions,
   207→                )
   208→                raise CommandError(
   209→                    f"rerun blocked: open backlog (objective: {objective_total}, "
   210→                    f"subjective: {subjective_total})",
   211→                    exit_code=1,
   212→                )
   213→
   214→    # Gate passed — clear stale for targeted dims
   215→    cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, dimensions=dimensions)
   216→    if cleared and state_file:
   217→        save_fn(state, state_file)
   218→        print(
   219→            colorize(
   220→                f"  Cleared stale review markers: {', '.join(sorted(cleared))}",
   221→                "cyan",
   222→            )
   223→        )
   224→
```

```
    72→class ScanStateContractError(ValueError):

    75→
    76→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    77→    """Best-effort clear for config.needs_rescan after a successful scan merge."""
    78→    if not config.get("needs_rescan"):
    79→        return
    80→    try:
    81→        config["needs_rescan"] = False
    82→        _save_config(config)
    83→    except OSError as exc:
    84→        _ = exc
    85→        config["needs_rescan"] = True

    88→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    89→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    90→    return bool(
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("overrides")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→        or plan.get("skipped")
    95→    )

    97→
    98→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    99→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
   100→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
   101→        return False
   102→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
   103→    if recon.resurfaced:
   104→        print(
   105→            colorize(
   106→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
   107→                "cyan",
   108→            )
   109→        )
   110→    return bool(recon.changes)
   111→

   113→def _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   114→    """Sync unscored subjective dimensions into the plan queue."""

   125→
   126→def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   127→    """Sync stale subjective dimensions (prune refreshed + inject stale) in plan queue."""
   128→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
   129→    if sync.pruned:
   130→        print(
   131→            colorize(
   132→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
   133→                "cyan",
   134→            )
   135→        )
   136→    if sync.injected:
   137→        print(
   138→            colorize(
   139→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} subjective dimension(s) queued for review.",
   140→                "cyan",
   141→            )
   142→        )
   143→    return bool(sync.changes)
   144→
   145→
   146→def _sync_auto_clusters(
   147→    plan: dict[str, object],
   148→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   149→    *,
   150→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
   151→    policy=None,
   152→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   153→) -> bool:
   154→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
   155→    return bool(auto_cluster_findings(
   156→        plan, state,
   157→        target_strict=target_strict,
   158→        policy=policy,
   159→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   160→    ))
   161→
   162→
   163→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   164→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   165→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   166→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   167→        return False
   168→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   169→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   170→        return False
   171→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   172→    if scores.strict is None:
   173→        return False
   174→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   175→        "strict": scores.strict,
   176→        "overall": scores.overall,
   177→        "objective": scores.objective,
   178→        "verified": scores.verified,
   179→    }
   180→    return True
   181→
   182→
   183→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   184→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   185→) -> bool:
   186→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   187→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   188→        return False
   189→
   190→    try:
   191→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_breakdown
   192→
   193→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   194→        queue_empty = breakdown.actionable == 0
   195→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   196→        logging.debug("Plan operation skipped: %s", exc)
   197→        return False
   198→    if not queue_empty:
   199→        return False
   200→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   201→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   202→    return True
   203→
   204→
   205→def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: "ScanRuntime") -> None:
   206→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   207→    try:
   208→        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   209→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   210→        dirty = False
   211→
   212→        if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   213→            dirty = True
   214→
   215→        unscored_changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   216→        if unscored_changed:
   217→            dirty = True
   218→            append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system",
   219→                             detail={"changes": True})
   220→
   221→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
   222→        _target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   223→
   224→        # Compute subjective visibility policy once for consistent gating
   225→        from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_subjective_visibility
   226→        _policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   227→            runtime.state,
   228→            target_strict=_target_strict,
   229→            plan=plan,
   230→        )
   231→
   232→        # Detect cycle completion: plan_start_scores is empty when the
   233→        # previous cycle drained the queue and revealed scores.  In that
   234→        # case stale subjective dimensions should be prioritized over new
   235→        # objective findings so the user reviews before a new cycle begins.
   236→        _cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   237→
   238→        stale_changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
   239→            plan, runtime.state,
   240→            lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
   241→                p, s, policy=_policy, cycle_just_completed=_cycle_just_completed,
   242→            ),
   243→        )
   244→        if stale_changed:
   245→            dirty = True
   246→            append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system",
   247→                             detail={"changes": True})
   248→
   249→        auto_changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   250→            plan, runtime.state, target_strict=_target_strict, policy=_policy,
   251→            cycle_just_completed=_cycle_just_completed,
   252→        )
   253→        if auto_changed:
   254→            dirty = True
   255→            append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system",
   256→                             detail={"changes": True})
   257→
   258→        triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, runtime.state)
   259→        if triage_sync.changes:
   260→            dirty = True
   261→            if triage_sync.injected:
   262→                print(
   263→                    colorize(
   264→                        "  Plan: planning mode needed — review findings changed since last triage.",
   265→                        "cyan",
   266→                    )
   267→                )
   268→                append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system",
   269→                                 detail={"injected": True})
   270→
   271→        checkpoint_sync = sync_score_checkpoint_needed(plan, runtime.state, policy=_policy)
   272→        if checkpoint_sync.changes:
   273→            dirty = True
   274→            append_log_entry(plan, "sync_score_checkpoint", actor="system",
   275→                             detail={"injected": True})
   276→
   277→        create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, runtime.state, policy=_policy)
   278→        if create_plan_sync.changes:
   279→            dirty = True
   280→            if create_plan_sync.injected:
   281→                print(
   282→                    colorize(
   283→                        "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   284→                        "cyan",
   285→                    )
   286→                )
   287→                append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system",
   288→                                 detail={"injected": True})
   289→
   290→        seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, runtime.state)
   291→        if seeded:
   292→            dirty = True
   293→            append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system",
   294→                             detail={})
   295→        # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   296→        # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   297→        if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
   298→            dirty = True
   299→            append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system",
   300→                             detail={})
   301→
   302→        if dirty:
   303→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   304→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   305→        logging.debug("Plan operation skipped: %s", exc)
   306→
   307→
   308→def _state_subjective_assessments(
   309→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   310→) -> dict[str, object]:
   311→    """Return normalized subjective assessment store from state."""
   312→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   313→    if isinstance(assessments, dict):
   314→        return assessments
   315→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   316→        "state.subjective_assessments must be an object; rerun with a valid state file"
   317→    )
   318→
   319→
   320→def _state_lang_capabilities(
   321→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   322→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   323→    """Return normalized language capability map from state."""
   324→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
   325→    if capabilities is None:
   326→        normalized: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   327→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
   328→        return normalized
   329→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict):
   330→        return capabilities
   331→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   332→        "state.lang_capabilities must be an object when present"
   333→    )
   334→
   335→
   336→def _state_findings(state: state_mod.StateModel) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   337→    """Return normalized finding map from state."""
   338→    findings = state.get("findings")
   339→    if isinstance(findings, dict):
   340→        return findings
   341→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   342→        "state.findings must be an object; state file appears corrupted"
   343→    )
   344→
   345→
   346→def _subjective_reset_dimensions(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   347→    """Resolve subjective dimensions that should reset on scan baseline reset."""
   348→    return resettable_default_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name)
   349→
   350→
   351→@dataclass
   352→class ScanRuntime:
   353→    """Resolved runtime context for a single scan invocation."""
   354→
   355→    args: argparse.Namespace
   356→    state_path: Path | None
   357→    state: state_mod.StateModel
   358→    path: Path
   359→    config: dict[str, object]
   360→    lang: LangRun | None
   361→    lang_label: str
   362→    profile: str
   363→    effective_include_slow: bool
   364→    zone_overrides: dict[str, object] | None
   365→    reset_subjective_count: int = 0
   366→    expired_manual_override_count: int = 0
   367→    coverage_warnings: list[DetectorCoverageRecord] = field(default_factory=list)
   368→
   369→
   370→@dataclass
   371→class ScanMergeResult:
   372→    """State merge outputs and previous score snapshots."""
   373→
   374→    diff: dict[str, object]
   375→    prev_overall: float | None
   376→    prev_objective: float | None
   377→    prev_strict: float | None
   378→    prev_verified: float | None
   379→    prev_dim_scores: dict[str, object]
   380→
   381→
   382→@dataclass
   383→class ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   384→    """Noise budget settings and hidden finding counts for this scan."""
   385→
   386→    noise_budget: int
   387→    global_noise_budget: int
   388→    budget_warning: str | None
   389→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int]
   390→    hidden_total: int
   391→
   392→
   393→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   394→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   395→    config: dict[str, object],
   396→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   397→    lang: LangRun | None,
   398→) -> LangRun | None:
   399→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   400→    if not lang:
   401→        return None
   402→
   403→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   404→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   405→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   406→        lang,
   407→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   408→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   409→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   410→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   411→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   412→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   413→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   414→        ),
   415→    )
   416→
   417→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   418→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   419→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   420→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   421→    }
   422→    return runtime_lang
   423→
   424→
   425→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   426→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   427→    *,
   428→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   429→) -> int:
   430→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   431→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   432→
   433→    reset_keys = {
   434→        key.strip()
   435→        for key in assessments
   436→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   437→    }
   438→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   439→
   440→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   441→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   442→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   443→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   444→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   445→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   446→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   447→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   448→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   449→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   450→            payload.pop("components", None)
   451→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   452→            continue
   453→        assessments[key] = {
   454→            "score": 0.0,
   455→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   456→            "assessed_at": now,
   457→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   458→            "placeholder": True,
   459→        }
   460→    return len(reset_keys)
   461→
   462→
   463→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   464→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   465→) -> int:
   466→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   467→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   468→
   469→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   470→    expired = 0
   471→    for payload in assessments.values():
   472→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   473→            continue
   474→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   475→            continue
   476→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   477→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   478→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   479→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   480→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   481→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   482→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   483→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   484→        payload.pop("components", None)
   485→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   486→        expired += 1
   487→    return expired
   488→
   489→
   490→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   491→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   492→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   493→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   494→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   495→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   496→    path = Path(args.path)
   497→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   498→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   499→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   500→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   501→        state
   502→    )
   503→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   504→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   505→            state,
   506→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   507→        )
   508→
   509→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   510→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   511→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   512→
   513→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   514→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   515→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   516→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   517→
   518→    return ScanRuntime(
   519→        args=args,
   520→        state_path=state_file,
   521→        state=state,
   522→        path=path,
   523→        config=config,
   524→        lang=lang,
   525→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   526→        profile=profile,
   527→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   528→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   529→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   530→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   531→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   532→    )
   533→
   534→
   535→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   536→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   537→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   538→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   539→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   540→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   541→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   542→        return findings
   543→
   544→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   545→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   546→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   547→    )
   548→    if not stale:
   549→        return findings
   550→
   551→    augmented = list(findings)
   552→    augmented.extend(stale)
   553→    for stale_finding in stale:
   554→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   555→    return augmented
   556→
   557→
   558→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   559→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   560→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   561→    *,
   562→    decay_scans: int,
   563→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   564→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   565→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   566→        findings,
   567→        state=runtime.state,
   568→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   569→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   570→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   571→    )
   572→
   573→
   574→def run_scan_generation(
   575→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   576→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   577→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   578→    enable_file_cache()
   579→    enable_parse_cache()
   580→    try:
   581→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   582→            runtime.path,
   583→            lang=runtime.lang,
   584→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   585→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   586→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   587→                profile=runtime.profile,
   588→            ),
   589→        )
   590→    finally:
   591→        disable_parse_cache()
   592→        disable_file_cache()
   593→
   594→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   595→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   596→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   597→    )
   598→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   599→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   600→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   601→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   602→    )
   603→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   604→        findings,
   605→        runtime,
   606→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   607→    )
   608→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   609→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   610→
   611→
   612→def merge_scan_results(
   613→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   614→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   615→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   616→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   617→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   618→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   619→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   620→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   621→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   622→
   623→    if not path_changed:
   624→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   625→    else:
   626→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   627→    prev_dim_scores = (
   628→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   629→    )
   630→
   631→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   632→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   633→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   634→
   635→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   636→
   637→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   638→        runtime.state,
   639→        findings,
   640→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   641→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   642→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   643→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   644→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   645→            potentials=potentials,
   646→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   647→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   648→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   649→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   650→        ),
   651→    )
   652→
   653→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   654→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   655→    )
   656→    state_mod.save_state(
   657→        runtime.state,
   658→        runtime.state_path,
   659→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   660→    )
   661→
   662→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   663→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   664→
   665→    return ScanMergeResult(
   666→        diff=diff,
   667→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   668→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   669→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   670→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   671→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   672→    )
   673→
   674→
   675→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   676→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   677→    config: dict[str, object],
   678→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   679→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   680→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   681→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   682→    )
   683→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   684→    open_findings = [
   685→        finding
   686→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   687→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   688→        ).values()
   689→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   690→    ]
   691→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   692→        open_findings,
   693→        budget=noise_budget,
   694→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   695→    )
   696→
   697→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   698→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   699→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   700→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   701→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   702→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   703→    )
   704→
   705→
   706→def persist_reminder_history(
   707→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   708→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   709→) -> None:
   710→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   711→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   712→        return
   713→
   714→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   715→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   716→    state_mod.save_state(
   717→        runtime.state,
   718→        runtime.state_path,
   719→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   720→    )
   721→
   722→
   723→__all__ = [
   724→    "ScanStateContractError",
   725→    "ScanMergeResult",
   726→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
```

```
   185→def _print_score_section(state, scores, plan, target_strict_score):
   186→    """Print score header: frozen plan-start or live score with queue breakdown."""
   187→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
   188→    breakdown = None
   189→    queue_remaining = 0
   190→    if plan_start_strict is not None:
   191→        try:
   192→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   193→            queue_remaining = breakdown.queue_total
   194→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   195→            logging.debug("Plan-aware queue count failed: %s", exc)
   196→            queue_remaining = 0
   197→    if plan_start_strict is not None and queue_remaining > 0:
   198→        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   199→            plan, queue_remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   200→        )
   201→    else:
   202→        for line, style in score_summary_lines(
   203→            overall_score=scores.overall,
   204→            objective_score=scores.objective,
   205→            strict_score=scores.strict,
   206→            verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
   207→            target_strict=target_strict_score,
   208→        ):
   209→            print(colorize(line, style))
   210→        # Show queue breakdown even without frozen score
   211→        if breakdown is None:
   212→            try:
   213→                breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   214→            except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   215→                breakdown = None
   216→        if breakdown is not None and breakdown.queue_total > 0:
   217→            block = format_queue_block(breakdown)
   218→            for text, style in block:
   219→                print(colorize(text, style))
   220→    return breakdown
   221→
   222→
   223→def _status_json_payload(
   224→    state: dict,
   225→    stats: dict,
   226→    dim_scores: dict,
   227→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
   228→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
   229→    suppression: dict,
   230→) -> dict:
   231→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   232→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   233→    open_scope = (
   234→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
   235→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
   236→        else None
   237→    )
   238→    return {
   239→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
   240→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
   241→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
   242→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   243→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   244→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
   245→        "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   246→        "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   247→        "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   248→        "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   249→        "stats": stats,
   250→        "open_scope": open_scope,
   251→        "suppression": suppression,
   252→        "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   253→        "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   254→    }
```

```
1→"""Shared render helpers for ``desloppify next`` terminal output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→

    13→
    14→_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS = {
    15→    "auto_fix": "Auto-fixable batch",

    17→    "refactor": "Refactor batch",
    18→    "manual_fix": "Grouped task",

    22→def scorecard_subjective(
    23→    state: dict,
    24→    dim_scores: dict,
    25→) -> list[dict]:
    26→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""

    30→        state,
    31→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    32→    )

    37→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:

    39→    return subjective_review_open_breakdown(findings_scoped)
    40→
    41→
    42→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    43→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    44→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify autofix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    45→
    46→
    47→def effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    48→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    49→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    50→        return "[review]"
    51→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    52→        return "[auto]"
    53→    return ""
    54→
    55→
    56→def render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    57→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    58→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    59→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    60→        for item in grouped_items:
    61→            confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    62→            tag = effort_tag(item)
    63→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    64→            print(
    65→                f"    [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    66→            )
    67→
    68→
    69→def render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
    70→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
    71→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
    72→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
    73→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
    74→    is_optional = bool(item.get("cluster_optional"))
    75→    if cluster_name == "auto/initial-review":
    76→        type_label = "Initial subjective review"
    77→    elif cluster_name == "auto/stale-review":
    78→        type_label = "Stale subjective review"
    79→    elif cluster_name == "auto/under-target-review":
    80→        type_label = "Optional re-review"
    81→    else:
    82→        type_label = _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
    83→    optional_tag = " — optional" if is_optional else ""
    84→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} findings{optional_tag})", "bold"))
    85→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    86→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
    87→
    88→    members = item.get("members", [])
    89→    if members:
    90→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
    91→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
    92→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
    93→            for filename, count in file_counts.most_common():
    94→                print(f"    {filename} ({count})")
    95→        else:
    96→            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
    97→            for filename, count in file_counts.most_common(3):
    98→                print(f"    {filename} ({count})")
    99→            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   100→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   101→
   102→        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   103→        for member in members[:3]:
   104→            print(f"    - {member.get('id', '')}")
   105→        if len(members) > 3:
   106→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   107→
   108→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   109→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   110→    if primary_command:
   111→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   112→
   113→    if is_optional:
   114→        print(colorize(f"\n  Skip:          desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   115→        print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10", "dim"))
   116→        print(
   117→            colorize(
   118→                f'  Resolve all:   desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --confirm',
   119→                "dim",
   120→            )
   121→        )
   122→        return
   123→
   124→    print(
   125→        colorize(
   126→            f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --confirm',
   127→            "dim",
   128→        )
   129→    )
   130→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10", "dim"))
   131→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   132→
   133→
   134→def render_gate_banner(gate_phase: str | None, *, item_count: int = 0) -> bool:
   135→    """No-op compatibility hook retained for previous queue gate semantics."""
   136→    del gate_phase, item_count
   137→    return False
   138→
   139→
   140→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   141→    del explain
   142→    total = queue.get("total", 0)
   143→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   144→    # When the only item is the "run scan" workflow action, show "Queue cleared"
   145→    # instead of a misleading "1 items" count.
   146→    if (
   147→        total == 1
   148→        and len(items) == 1
   149→        and items[0].get("id") == "workflow::run-scan"
   150→    ):
   151→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared (1 workflow step)", "bold"))
   152→    else:
   153→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {total} item{'s' if total != 1 else ''}", "bold"))
   154→
   155→
   156→def show_empty_queue(
   157→    queue: dict,
   158→    strict: float | None,
   159→    *,
   160→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   161→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   162→) -> bool:
   163→    del target_strict
   164→    if queue.get("items"):
   165→        return False
   166→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   167→        delta = round(strict - plan_start_strict, 1)
   168→        delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if abs(delta) >= 0.05 else ""
   169→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   170→        print(colorize(
   171→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   172→            "cyan",
   173→        ))
   174→        print(colorize(
   175→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   176→            "dim",
   177→        ))
   178→        return True
   179→
   180→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   181→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   182→    return True
   183→
   184→
   185→def render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   186→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   187→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   188→    tag = effort_tag(item)
   189→    tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
   190→    fid = item.get("id", "")
   191→    short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid
   192→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   193→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   194→
   195→
```
