```
1→"""Queue-order enforcement helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
     9→
    10→
    11→def _check_queue_order_guard(
    12→    state: dict,
    13→    patterns: list[str],
    14→    status: str,
    15→) -> bool:
    16→    """Warn and block if resolving items not at the front of the plan queue."""
    17→    if status != "fixed":
    18→        return False
    19→    try:
    20→        if not has_living_plan():
    21→            return False
    22→        plan = load_plan()
    23→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    24→        if not queue_order:
    25→            return False
    26→
    27→        result = build_work_queue(
    28→            state,
    29→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
    30→                plan=plan,
    31→                count=None,
    32→                include_subjective=True,
    33→            ),
    34→        )
    35→        items = result["items"]
    36→        if not plan.get("active_cluster"):
    37→            items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
    38→        if not items:
    39→            return False
    40→
    41→        front_item = items[0]
    42→        front_id = front_item["id"]
    43→
    44→        front_ids: set[str] = set()
    45→        if front_item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    46→            for member in front_item.get("members", []):
    47→                front_ids.add(member["id"])
    48→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    49→        else:
    50→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    51→
    52→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    53→        resolved_ids: set[str] = set()
    54→        for pattern in patterns:
    55→            if pattern in clusters:
    56→                resolved_ids.update(clusters[pattern].get("finding_ids", []))
    57→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    58→            else:
    59→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    60→
    61→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
    62→        resolved_ids = {
    63→            finding_id
    64→            for finding_id in resolved_ids
    65→            if finding_id in clusters
    66→            or (finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open")
    67→        }
    68→        if not resolved_ids:
    69→            return False
    70→
    71→        out_of_order = resolved_ids - front_ids
    72→        for cluster_id in list(out_of_order):
    73→            if cluster_id in clusters:
    74→                alive_members = {
    75→                    finding_id
    76→                    for finding_id in clusters[cluster_id].get("finding_ids", [])
    77→                    if finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open"
    78→                }
    79→                if alive_members and alive_members <= front_ids:
    80→                    out_of_order.discard(cluster_id)
    81→        if not out_of_order:
    82→            return False
    83→
    84→        print(colorize("\n  Queue order violation: these items are not next in the plan queue:\n", "yellow"))
    85→        for finding_id in sorted(out_of_order):
    86→            print(f"    {finding_id}")
    87→        print(colorize(f"\n  The current next item is: {front_id}", "dim"))
    88→        print(colorize("  Items must be resolved in plan order. If you need to reprioritize:", "dim"))
    89→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan move <pattern> --position top    # move to front", "dim"))
    90→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan skip <pattern> --reason '...'    # skip for now", "dim"))
    91→        print(colorize("    desloppify next                                  # see what's next\n", "dim"))
    92→        return True
    93→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    94→        return False
    95→
    96→
    97→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard"]
    98→
```

```
1→"""Runtime context helpers for command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    11→from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    12→
    13→
    14→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    15→class CommandRuntime:
    16→    """Explicit runtime dependencies shared by command handlers."""
    17→
    18→    config: dict[str, Any]
    19→    state: dict[str, Any]
    20→    state_path: Path | None
    21→
    22→
    23→def command_runtime(args) -> CommandRuntime:
    24→    """Return runtime context from explicit args.runtime or construct one."""
    25→    runtime = getattr(args, "runtime", None)
    26→    if isinstance(runtime, CommandRuntime):
    27→        return runtime
    28→
    29→    config = load_config()
    30→    state_file = state_path(args)
    31→    if isinstance(state_file, str):
    32→        state_file = Path(state_file)
    33→
    34→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    35→
    36→    return CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
    37→
    38→
    39→__all__ = ["CommandRuntime", "command_runtime"]
    40→
```

```
1→"""Scan preflight guard: warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_breakdown
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    10→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS, CommandError
    11→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    12→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
    13→
    14→
    15→def scan_queue_preflight(args) -> None:
    16→    """Warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
    17→    # CI profile always passes
    18→    if getattr(args, "profile", None) == "ci":
    19→        return
    20→
    21→    # --force-rescan with valid attestation bypasses
    22→    if getattr(args, "force_rescan", False):
    23→        attest = getattr(args, "attest", None) or ""
    24→        if "i understand" not in attest.lower():
    25→            raise CommandError(
    26→                '--force-rescan requires --attest "I understand this is not '
    27→                "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
    28→                'completion"'
    29→            )
    30→        print(
    31→            colorize(
    32→                "  --force-rescan: bypassing queue completion check. "
    33→                "Plan-start score will be reset.",
    34→                "yellow",
    35→            )
    36→        )
    37→        # Clear plan_start_scores
    38→        try:
    39→            plan = load_plan()
    40→            if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    41→                plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    42→                save_plan(plan)
    43→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    44→            logging.debug("Plan score cleanup skipped: %s", exc)
    45→        return
    46→
    47→    # No plan = no gate (first scan, or user never uses plan)
    48→    try:
    49→        plan = load_plan()
    50→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    51→        return
    52→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    53→        return  # No active cycle
    54→
    55→    # Count plan-aware remaining items, excluding workflow actions (e.g.
    56→    # "run scan") which would otherwise create a circular gate.
    57→    try:
    58→        state = state_mod.load_state(state_path(args))
    59→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
    60→        remaining = breakdown.actionable
    61→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    62→        return
    63→    if remaining <= 0:
    64→        return  # Queue clear, scan allowed
    65→
    66→    # GATE
    67→    raise CommandError(
    68→        f"{remaining} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''}"
    69→        " remaining in your queue.\n"
    70→        "  The intended workflow is to complete the queue before scanning.\n"
    71→        "  Work through items with `desloppify next`, then scan when clear.\n\n"
    72→        "  To force a rescan (resets your plan-start score):\n"
    73→        '    desloppify scan --force-rescan --attest "I understand this is not '
    74→        "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
    75→        'completion"'
    76→    )
    77→
    78→
    79→__all__ = ["scan_queue_preflight"]
    80→
```

```
1→"""Target-score normalization helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.text_api import is_numeric
     6→
     7→
     8→def coerce_target_score(value: object, *, fallback: float = 95.0) -> float:
     9→    """Normalize target score-like values to a safe [0, 100] float."""
    10→    if is_numeric(fallback):
    11→        fallback_value = float(fallback)
    12→    else:
    13→        fallback_value = 95.0
    14→
    15→    if is_numeric(value):
    16→        parsed = float(value)
    17→    elif isinstance(value, str):
    18→        text = value.strip()
    19→        if not text:
    20→            parsed = fallback_value
    21→        else:
    22→            try:
    23→                parsed = float(text)
    24→            except ValueError:
    25→                parsed = fallback_value
    26→    else:
    27→        parsed = fallback_value
    28→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, parsed))
    29→
    30→
    31→def target_strict_score_from_config(
    32→    config: dict | None, *, fallback: float = 95.0
    33→) -> float:
    34→    """Read and normalize target strict score from config."""
    35→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    36→        raw = config.get("target_strict_score", fallback)
    37→    else:
    38→        raw = fallback
    39→    return coerce_target_score(raw, fallback=fallback)
    40→
    41→
```

```
1→"""Shared triage guardrail helpers for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.display import short_finding_id
     8→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS, CommandError
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    11→    compute_new_finding_ids,
    12→    is_triage_stale,
    13→    load_plan,
    14→    triage_phase_banner,
    15→)
    16→
    17→
    18→@dataclass
    19→class TriageGuardrailResult:
    20→    """Structured result from triage staleness detection."""
    21→
    22→    is_stale: bool = False
    23→    new_ids: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    24→    _plan: dict | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    25→
    26→
    27→def triage_guardrail_status(
    28→    *,
    29→    plan: dict | None = None,
    30→    state: dict | None = None,
    31→) -> TriageGuardrailResult:
    32→    """Pure detection: is triage stale? Returns structured result, no side effects."""
    33→    try:
    34→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    35→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    36→        return TriageGuardrailResult()
    37→
    38→    resolved_state = state or {}
    39→
    40→    if not is_triage_stale(resolved_plan, resolved_state):
    41→        return TriageGuardrailResult(_plan=resolved_plan)
    42→
    43→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
    44→    if resolved_state:
    45→        new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(resolved_plan, resolved_state)
    46→
    47→    return TriageGuardrailResult(is_stale=True, new_ids=new_ids, _plan=resolved_plan)
    48→
    49→
    50→def print_triage_guardrail_info(
    51→    *,
    52→    plan: dict | None = None,
    53→    state: dict | None = None,
    54→) -> bool:
    55→    """Print yellow info banner if triage is stale. Returns True if banner was shown."""
    56→    result = triage_guardrail_status(plan=plan, state=state)
    57→    if not result.is_stale:
    58→        return False
    59→
    60→    if result.new_ids:
    61→        print(colorize(
    62→            f"  {len(result.new_ids)} new review finding(s) not yet triaged.",
    63→            "yellow",
    64→        ))
    65→
    66→    if result._plan is not None:
    67→        banner = triage_phase_banner(result._plan)
    68→        if banner:
    69→            print(colorize(f"  {banner}", "yellow"))
    70→
    71→    return True
    72→
    73→
    74→def require_triage_current_or_exit(
    75→    *,
    76→    state: dict,
    77→    bypass: bool = False,
    78→    attest: str = "",
    79→) -> None:
    80→    """Gate: exit(1) if triage is stale and not bypassed. Name signals the exit."""
    81→    result = triage_guardrail_status(state=state)
    82→    if not result.is_stale:
    83→        return
    84→
    85→    if bypass and attest and len(attest.strip()) >= 30:
    86→        print(colorize(
    87→            "  Triage guardrail bypassed with attestation.",
    88→            "yellow",
    89→        ))
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    new_ids = result.new_ids
    93→    lines = [
    94→        f"BLOCKED: {len(new_ids) or 'some'} new review finding(s) have not been triaged."
    95→    ]
    96→    if new_ids:
    97→        for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
    98→            f = state.get("findings", {}).get(fid, {})
    99→            lines.append(f"    * [{short_finding_id(fid)}] {f.get('summary', '')}")
   100→        if len(new_ids) > 5:
   101→            lines.append(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more")
   102→    lines.append("")
   103→    lines.append("  NEXT STEP: desloppify plan triage")
   104→    lines.append("  (Review new findings, then either --confirm-existing or re-plan.)")
   105→    lines.append("")
   106→    lines.append("  View new items:  desloppify plan queue --sort recent")
   107→    lines.append('  To bypass: --force-resolve --attest "I understand the plan may be stale..."')
   108→    raise CommandError("\n".join(lines))
   109→
   110→
   111→__all__ = [
   112→    "TriageGuardrailResult",
   113→    "print_triage_guardrail_info",
   114→    "require_triage_current_or_exit",
   115→    "triage_guardrail_status",
   116→]
   117→
```

```
    38→def _scorecard_subjective(

    45→
    46→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    47→    state: dict,
    48→    dim_scores: dict,
    49→    *,
    50→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    51→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    52→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    53→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    54→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    55→        if entry.get("placeholder"):

    57→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))

    62→                    strict_val,
    63→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    64→                )
    65→            )
    66→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    67→    return low

    70→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    97→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    98→        if active_cluster:

   100→    return effective_cluster

   123→    ]

   165→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   166→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   167→        return raw_potentials or None
   168→
   169→
   170→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   171→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
   172→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
   173→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
   174→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
   175→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
   176→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   177→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   178→
   179→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
   180→
   181→    # Load the living plan
   182→    plan = load_plan()
   183→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   184→    if (
   185→        plan.get("queue_order")
   186→        or plan.get("overrides")
   187→        or plan.get("clusters")
   188→    ):
   189→        plan_data = plan
   190→
   191→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   192→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   193→        plan_data,
   194→        cluster_arg=cluster_arg,
   195→        scope=scope,
   196→    )
   197→
   198→    queue = build_work_queue(
   199→        state,
   200→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   201→            count=count,
   202→            scope=scope,
   203→            status=status,
   204→            include_subjective=True,
   205→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   206→            explain=explain,
   207→            plan=plan_data,
   208→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   209→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   210→        ),
   211→    )
   212→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   213→
   214→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   215→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   216→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   217→        state,
   218→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   219→    )
   220→
   221→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   222→        queue=queue,
   223→        items=items,
   224→        state=state,
   225→        narrative=narrative,
   226→        plan_data=plan_data,
   227→    )
   228→    write_query(payload)
   229→
   230→    if _emit_requested_output(args, payload, items):
   231→        return
   232→
   233→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   234→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   235→        state.get("findings", {}),
   236→        state.get("scan_path"),
   237→    )
   238→
   239→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   240→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   241→        state=state,
   242→        plan_data=plan_data,
   243→    )
   244→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   245→
   246→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   247→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   248→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(
   249→        queue,
   250→        strict_score,
   251→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   252→        target_strict=target_strict,
   253→    ):
   254→        return
   255→
   256→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   257→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   258→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   259→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   260→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   261→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   262→    )
   263→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   264→    next_render_mod.render_uncommitted_reminder(plan_data)
   265→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   266→        state,
   267→        dim_scores,
   268→        findings_scoped,
   269→        strict_score=strict_score,
   270→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   271→        queue_total=queue_total,
   272→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   273→        breakdown=breakdown,
   274→    )
   275→    print()
   276→
   277→
   278→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   279→
```

```
1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_triage_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_new_finding_ids, load_plan
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    13→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    14→
    15→
    16→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    17→    if len(text) <= width:
    18→        return text
    19→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    20→
    21→
    22→def _print_queue_header(
    23→    *,
    24→    items: list[dict],
    25→    include_skipped: bool,
    26→    plan: dict,
    27→    new_count: int = 0,
    28→) -> None:
    29→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
    30→        QueueBreakdown,
    31→        format_queue_headline,
    32→    )
    33→
    34→    total = len(items)
    35→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    36→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    37→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    38→
    39→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    40→    subjective = sum(
    41→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    42→    )
    43→
    44→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    45→    queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    46→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    47→    plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    48→
    49→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    50→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    51→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    52→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    53→        subjective=subjective,
    54→    )
    55→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    56→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    57→    print(colorize(f"\n  {headline}{new_suffix}", "bold"))
    58→
    59→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster")
    60→    if focus:
    61→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    62→
    63→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    64→        return
    65→    if not plan_skipped_total:
    66→        return
    67→    print(
    68→        colorize(
    69→            f"  ({plan_skipped_total} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_total != 1 else ''}"
    70→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    71→            "dim",
    72→        )
    73→    )
    74→
    75→
    76→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    77→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    78→        return items[:top]
    79→    return items
    80→
    81→
    82→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    83→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    84→    _new = new_ids or set()
    85→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
    86→        pos = str(idx)
    87→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    88→
    89→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
    90→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
    91→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
    92→            conf_str = "—"
    93→            detector = "planning"
    94→            summary = f"[TRIAGE] {item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
    95→            cluster_name = ""
    96→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
    97→            conf_str = "—"
    98→            detector = "workflow"
    99→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   100→            cluster_name = ""
   101→        elif kind == "cluster":
   102→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "high")
   103→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   104→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   105→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   106→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   107→                if m.get("id") in _new
   108→            )
   109→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   110→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   111→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   112→        else:
   113→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   114→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   115→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   116→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   117→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   118→
   119→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   120→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   121→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   122→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   123→    return rows
   124→
   125→
   126→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   127→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   128→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   129→    state = runtime.state
   130→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   131→        return
   132→
   133→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   134→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   135→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   136→
   137→    plan = load_plan()
   138→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   139→
   140→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
   141→    if not cluster_filter:
   142→        active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   143→        if active_cluster:
   144→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   145→
   146→    queue = build_work_queue(
   147→        state,
   148→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   149→            count=None,
   150→            status="open",
   151→            include_subjective=True,
   152→            plan=plan,
   153→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   154→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   155→        ),
   156→    )
   157→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   158→    # Collapse auto-clusters into display meta-items
   159→    if plan and not effective_cluster and not plan.get("active_cluster"):
   160→        items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   161→
   162→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   163→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   164→    # Merge review-based new finding IDs (since last triage)
   165→    review_new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   166→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   167→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   168→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   169→
   170→    if sort_by == "recent":
   171→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   172→
   173→    _print_queue_header(
   174→        items=items,
   175→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   176→        plan=plan,
   177→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   178→    )
   179→
   180→    if not items:
   181→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   182→        return
   183→
   184→    # Determine which items to show
   185→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   186→    headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   187→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   188→
   189→    print()
   190→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   191→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   192→
   193→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   194→        remaining = len(items) - top
   195→        print(colorize(
   196→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   197→        ))
   198→    print()
   199→
   200→
   201→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   202→
```

```
1→"""Scope and queue helpers for show command selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.core import registry as registry_mod
    10→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    build_work_queue,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    18→class ResolvedEntity:
    19→    """Classifies what the user's show pattern refers to."""
    20→
    21→    kind: str  # "dimension", "special_view", "file_or_pattern"
    22→    pattern: str  # Original user pattern
    23→    display_name: str  # Human-readable label
    24→    detectors: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # Detector names (for dimension kind)
    25→    is_subjective: bool = False  # Whether this is a subjective dimension
    26→
    27→
    28→def _resolve_special_view(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    29→    if lowered not in ("concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"):
    30→        return None
    31→    return ResolvedEntity(
    32→        kind="special_view",
    33→        pattern=pattern,
    34→        display_name=pattern,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→
    38→def _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    39→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    40→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered) or lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    41→    if not detectors:
    42→        return None
    43→    dim_display = pattern
    44→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    45→        dim_lowered = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    46→        if dim.name.lower() == lowered or dim_lowered == lowered:
    47→            dim_display = dim.name
    48→            break
    49→    return ResolvedEntity(
    50→        kind="dimension",
    51→        pattern=pattern,
    52→        display_name=dim_display,
    53→        detectors=tuple(detectors),
    54→        is_subjective=False,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→def _resolve_subjective_dimension(
    59→    pattern: str,
    60→    lowered: str,
    61→    state: dict,
    62→) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    63→    display_name = scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(lowered)
    64→    if not display_name:
    65→        for key in state.get("dimension_scores") or {}:
    66→            if key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
    67→                display_name = key
    68→                break
    69→    if not display_name:
    70→        return None
    71→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, display_name)
    72→    is_subj = "subjective_assessment" in (
    73→        dim_data.get("detectors", {}) if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else {}
    74→    )
    75→    return ResolvedEntity(
    76→        kind="dimension",
    77→        pattern=pattern,
    78→        display_name=display_name,
    79→        detectors=(),
    80→        is_subjective=is_subj,
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def resolve_entity(pattern: str, state: dict) -> ResolvedEntity:
    85→    """Classify a user pattern as a dimension, special view, or passthrough.
    86→
    87→    Resolution priority:
    88→    1. Special views: "concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"
    89→    2. Mechanical dimension name (from DIMENSIONS)
    90→    3. Subjective dimension name (from DISPLAY_NAMES / dimension_scores)
    91→    4. Everything else: file_or_pattern passthrough
    92→    """
    93→    lowered = pattern.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    94→
    95→    # 1. Special views
    96→    special_view = _resolve_special_view(pattern, lowered)
    97→    if special_view is not None:
    98→        return special_view
    99→
   100→    # 2. Mechanical dimension (via DIMENSIONS list)
   101→    mechanical = _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern, lowered)
   102→    if mechanical is not None:
   103→        return mechanical
   104→
   105→    # 3. Subjective dimension (via DISPLAY_NAMES or dimension_scores)
   106→    subjective = _resolve_subjective_dimension(pattern, lowered, state)
   107→    if subjective is not None:
   108→        return subjective
   109→
   110→    # 4. Everything else
   111→    return ResolvedEntity(
   112→        kind="file_or_pattern",
   113→        pattern=pattern,
   114→        display_name=pattern,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→
   118→def _build_dimension_lookup() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   119→    """Build a map from dimension name/key (lowered) to detector names."""
   120→    lookup: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   121→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
   122→        detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   123→        lookup[dim.name.lower()] = detectors
   124→        # Also index by underscore key: "file_health" -> detectors
   125→        key = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   126→        if key not in lookup:
   127→            lookup[key] = detectors
   128→    # Also add DISPLAY_NAMES reverse lookup (e.g. "abstraction_fit" -> "Abstraction Fit")
   129→    for key, display in scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   130→        normalized_key = key.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   131→        normalized_display = display.lower()
   132→        # Find which dimension this belongs to via get_dimension_for_detector or direct name match
   133→        for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
   134→            dim_lower = dim.name.lower()
   135→            if normalized_display == dim_lower or normalized_key == dim_lower.replace(" ", "_"):
   136→                detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   137→                if normalized_key not in lookup:
   138→                    lookup[normalized_key] = detectors
   139→                if normalized_display not in lookup:
   140→                    lookup[normalized_display] = detectors
   141→    return lookup
   142→
   143→
   144→def _lookup_dimension_score(
   145→    state: dict, display_name: str,
   146→) -> tuple[dict, str]:
   147→    """Find dimension_scores entry with case-insensitive fallback.
   148→
   149→    Returns (dim_data_dict, resolved_display_name).
   150→    """
   151→    lowered = display_name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   152→    dim_data = (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).get(display_name, {})
   153→    if not dim_data:
   154→        for ds_key, ds_val in (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).items():
   155→            if ds_key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
   156→                dim_data = ds_val
   157→                display_name = ds_key
   158→                break
   159→    return dim_data, display_name
   160→
   161→
   162→def _detector_names_hint() -> str:
   163→    """Return a compact list of detector names for the help message."""
   164→    names = getattr(registry_mod, "DISPLAY_ORDER", [])
   165→    if names:
   166→        return ", ".join(names[:10]) + (", ..." if len(names) > 10 else "")
   167→    return "smells, structural, security, review, ..."
   168→
   169→
   170→def resolve_show_scope(args) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str, str | None]:
   171→    """Resolve scope/pattern/status for a show invocation."""
   172→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   173→    pattern = args.pattern
   174→    status_filter = "open" if chronic else getattr(args, "status", "open")
   175→    if chronic:
   176→        scope = pattern
   177→        pattern = pattern or "<chronic>"
   178→        return True, pattern, status_filter, scope
   179→    if not pattern:
   180→        print(
   181→            colorize(
   182→                "Pattern required (or use --chronic). Try: desloppify show --help",
   183→                "yellow",
   184→            )
   185→        )
   186→        return False, None, status_filter, ""
   187→    return True, pattern, status_filter, pattern
   188→
   189→
   190→def load_matches(
   191→    state: dict,
   192→    *,
   193→    scope: str | None,
   194→    status_filter: str,
   195→    chronic: bool,
   196→) -> list[dict]:
   197→    """Load matching findings from the ranked queue."""
   198→    queue = build_work_queue(
   199→        state,
   200→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   201→            count=None,
   202→            scope=scope,
   203→            status=status_filter,
   204→            include_subjective=False,
   205→            chronic=chronic,
   206→        ),
   207→    )
   208→    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
   209→
   210→
   211→def resolve_noise(config: dict, matches: list[dict], *, no_budget: bool = False):
   212→    """Apply detector/global noise budget to show matches.
   213→
   214→    When *no_budget* is True, all matches are surfaced (nothing hidden).
   215→    """
   216→    if no_budget:
   217→        return (
   218→            matches,
   219→            {},
   220→            0,
   221→            0,
   222→            None,
   223→        )
   224→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   225→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   226→    )
   227→    surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   228→        matches,
   229→        budget=noise_budget,
   230→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   231→    )
   232→    return (
   233→        surfaced_matches,
   234→        hidden_by_detector,
   235→        noise_budget,
   236→        global_noise_budget,
   237→        budget_warning,
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→__all__ = [
   242→    "ResolvedEntity",
   243→    "_detector_names_hint",
   244→    "_lookup_dimension_score",
   245→    "load_matches",
   246→    "resolve_entity",
   247→    "resolve_noise",
   248→    "resolve_show_scope",
   249→]
   250→
```

```
     3→Covers:
     4→- Fix 1: auto_resolve_disappeared resolves out-of-scope findings
     5→- Fix 2: queue counting functions pass scan_path
     6→- Fix 3: compute_subjective_visibility respects scan_path + plan
     7→- Fix 4a: workflow::run-scan synthetic item
     8→- Fix 6: queue_guard passes scan_path
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→from unittest.mock import patch
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.merge_findings import auto_resolve_disappeared
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import compute_subjective_visibility
    17→
    18→
    19→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    20→# Helpers
    21→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    22→
    23→def _finding(
    24→    fid: str,
    25→    detector: str = "unused",
    26→    status: str = "open",
    27→    file: str = "src/foo.ts",
    28→    suppressed: bool = False,
    29→) -> dict:
    30→    f: dict = {
    31→        "id": fid,
    32→        "detector": detector,
    33→        "status": status,
    34→        "file": file,
    35→    }
    36→    if suppressed:
    37→        f["suppressed"] = True
    38→    return f
    39→
    40→
    41→def _state_with_findings(*findings: dict) -> dict:
    42→    return {
    43→        "findings": {f["id"]: f for f in findings},
    44→        "scan_count": 5,
    45→    }
    46→
    47→
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→# Fix 1: auto_resolve_disappeared resolves out-of-scope findings
    50→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    51→
    52→class TestAutoResolveOutOfScope:
    53→    def test_out_of_scope_findings_are_resolved(self):
    54→        """Findings outside scan_path are auto-resolved, not skipped."""
    55→        existing = {
    56→            "f1": {
    57→                "id": "f1", "status": "open", "file": "supabase/fn.ts",
    58→                "detector": "unused",
    59→            },
    60→            "f2": {
    61→                "id": "f2", "status": "open", "file": "src/app.ts",
    62→                "detector": "unused",
    63→            },
    64→        }
    65→        resolved, _lang, out_of_scope, detectors = auto_resolve_disappeared(
    66→            existing,
    67→            current_ids=set(),
    68→            suspect_detectors=set(),
    69→            now="2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
    70→            lang=None,
    71→            scan_path="src",
    72→        )
    73→        # f1 is out of scope and should be auto-resolved
    74→        assert existing["f1"]["status"] == "auto_resolved"
    75→        assert "Out of current scan scope" in existing["f1"]["note"]
    76→        assert out_of_scope == 1
    77→        # f2 is in scope and should also be resolved (disappeared from scan)
    78→        assert existing["f2"]["status"] == "auto_resolved"
    79→        assert resolved == 1  # f2 counts toward normal resolved
    80→        assert "unused" in detectors
    81→
    82→    def test_out_of_scope_resolution_attestation(self):
    83→        """Out-of-scope findings get scan_verified attestation with scope note."""
    84→        existing = {
    85→            "f1": {
    86→                "id": "f1", "status": "open", "file": "supabase/fn.ts",
    87→                "detector": "smells",
    88→            },
    89→        }
    90→        auto_resolve_disappeared(
    91→            existing,
    92→            current_ids=set(),
    93→            suspect_detectors=set(),
    94→            now="2026-03-01T00:00:00+00:00",
    95→            lang=None,
    96→            scan_path="src",
    97→        )
    98→        attestation = existing["f1"]["resolution_attestation"]
    99→        assert attestation["kind"] == "scan_verified"
   100→        assert "scan_path: src" in attestation["text"]
   101→

   104→        existing = {

   162→
   163→class TestQueueCountingScanPath:
   164→    def test_queue_count_respects_scan_path_from_state(self):
   165→        """build_work_queue auto-reads scan_path from state and filters findings."""

   247→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   248→# Fix 3: compute_subjective_visibility respects scan_path + plan
   249→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   250→
   251→class TestSubjectivePolicyScanPathAndPlan:
   252→    def test_scan_path_excludes_out_of_scope_findings(self):
   253→        """Findings outside scan_path don't count toward objective backlog."""
   254→        state = _state_with_findings(
   255→            _finding("f1", "unused", file="src/app.ts"),
   256→            _finding("f2", "unused", file="supabase/fn.ts"),
   257→            _finding("f3", "unused", file="src/lib.ts"),
   258→        )
   259→        # Without scan_path: all 3 count
   260→        policy_all = compute_subjective_visibility(state)
   261→        assert policy_all.objective_count == 3
   262→
   263→        # With scan_path: only 2 in src/ count
   264→        policy_scoped = compute_subjective_visibility(state, scan_path="src")
   265→        assert policy_scoped.objective_count == 2
   266→        assert policy_scoped.has_objective_backlog is True
   267→
   268→    def test_scan_path_drains_backlog_correctly(self):
   269→        """When all in-scope findings are resolved, backlog is drained even
   270→        if out-of-scope findings remain open."""
   271→        state = _state_with_findings(
   272→            _finding("f1", "unused", file="supabase/fn.ts"),
   273→            _finding("f2", "unused", file="supabase/other.ts"),
   274→        )
   275→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, scan_path="src")
   276→        assert policy.objective_count == 0
   277→        assert policy.has_objective_backlog is False
   278→
   279→    def test_plan_skipped_findings_excluded(self):
   280→        """Findings in plan['skipped'] don't count toward objective backlog."""
   281→        state = _state_with_findings(
   282→            _finding("f1", "unused", file="src/a.ts"),
   283→            _finding("f2", "unused", file="src/b.ts"),
   284→            _finding("f3", "unused", file="src/c.ts"),
   285→        )
   286→        plan = {"skipped": {"f1": {"kind": "temporary"}, "f2": {"kind": "permanent"}}}
   287→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, plan=plan)
   288→        assert policy.objective_count == 1  # only f3
   289→
   290→    def test_scan_path_and_plan_combined(self):
   291→        """Both filters applied together: scope + skipped."""
   292→        state = _state_with_findings(
   293→            _finding("f1", "unused", file="src/a.ts"),     # in scope, not skipped → counts
   294→            _finding("f2", "unused", file="src/b.ts"),     # in scope, skipped → excluded
   295→            _finding("f3", "unused", file="other/c.ts"),   # out of scope → excluded
   296→        )
   297→        plan = {"skipped": {"f2": {"kind": "temporary"}}}
   298→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, scan_path="src", plan=plan)
   299→        assert policy.objective_count == 1
   300→        assert policy.has_objective_backlog is True
   301→
   302→    def test_no_params_backward_compat(self):
   303→        """Without new params, behavior is unchanged (all findings counted)."""
   304→        state = _state_with_findings(
   305→            _finding("f1", "unused", file="anywhere/a.ts"),
   306→        )
   307→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state)
   308→        assert policy.objective_count == 1
   309→

   405→    def test_run_scan_does_not_block_scan_preflight(self):

   456→class TestQueueGuardScanPath:
   457→    def test_queue_guard_respects_scan_path_from_state(self):
   458→        """_check_queue_order_guard uses build_work_queue which auto-reads
   459→        scan_path from state, so out-of-scope items don't appear in the queue."""
   460→        from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.queue_guard import _check_queue_order_guard
   461→
   462→        state = {

   465→            },

   467→        }
   468→        mock_result = {
   469→            "total": 1,
   470→            "items": [{"id": "f1", "kind": "finding"}],
   471→            "grouped": {},
   472→            "new_ids": set(),
   473→        }
   474→        with (
   475→            patch("desloppify.app.commands.resolve.queue_guard.has_living_plan", return_value=True),
   476→            patch("desloppify.app.commands.resolve.queue_guard.load_plan", return_value={
   477→                "queue_order": ["f1"],
   478→                "skipped": {},
   479→            }),
   480→            patch(
   481→                "desloppify.app.commands.resolve.queue_guard.build_work_queue",
   482→                return_value=mock_result,
   483→            ) as mock_build,
   484→        ):
   485→            # Should not raise — f1 is at front of queue
   486→            result = _check_queue_order_guard(state, ["f1"], "fixed")
   487→            assert result is False
   488→            # Verify build_work_queue was called (scan_path resolved internally)
   489→            mock_build.assert_called_once()
```

```
1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    12→    _current_stale_ids,
    13→    current_under_target_ids,
    14→    current_unscored_ids,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    17→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    18→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    19→    compute_subjective_visibility,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    22→
    23→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    24→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    25→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    26→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    27→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    28→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    29→_UNDER_TARGET_KEY = "subjective::under-target"
    30→_UNDER_TARGET_NAME = "auto/under-target-review"
    31→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    32→
    33→
    34→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35→# Grouping key computation
    36→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    37→
    38→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    39→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    40→
    41→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    42→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    43→    """
    44→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    45→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    46→    if kind:
    47→        return kind
    48→
    49→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    50→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    51→    parts = fid.split("::")
    52→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    53→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    54→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    55→        candidate = parts[-1]
    56→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    57→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    58→            return candidate
    59→    return None
    60→
```

```
1→"""Public plan API facade.
     2→
```

```
   120→
   121→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
   122→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
   123→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   124→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   125→    has_triage = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   126→    if not has_triage:
   127→        return ""
   128→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   129→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   130→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
   131→    if completed:
   132→        return (
   133→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete) — "
   134→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   135→        )
   136→    return (
   137→        "TRIAGE MODE — review findings need analysis before fixing. "
   138→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"
   139→    )
   140→
```

```
    24→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:

    27→    objective_score = get_objective_score(state)

    33→        and strict_score is not None

    36→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    37→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    38→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    39→        )
    40→    elif overall_score is not None:
    41→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    42→    else:
    43→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    44→
    45→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    46→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    47→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    48→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    49→
    50→    lines = [
```

```
1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     9→    QueueBuildOptions,
    10→    WorkQueueResult,
    11→    build_work_queue,
    12→)
    13→
    14→
    15→def _subjective_threshold(state: dict[str, Any], *, default: float = 95.0) -> float:
    16→    config = state.get("config", {})
    17→    raw_target = default
    18→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    19→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    20→    try:
    21→        value = float(raw_target)
    22→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    23→        value = default
    24→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    28→class OpenPlanQueuePolicy:
    29→    count: int | None = None
    30→    scan_path: str | None = None
    31→    include_subjective: bool = True
    32→
    33→
    34→def build_open_plan_queue(
    35→    state: dict[str, Any],
    36→    policy: OpenPlanQueuePolicy | None = None,
    37→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    38→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    39→    policy = policy or OpenPlanQueuePolicy()
    40→    # When policy.scan_path is explicitly set, override the auto-default.
    41→    # Otherwise let QueueBuildOptions read from state automatically.
    42→    scan_path_kwarg: dict = (
    43→        {"scan_path": policy.scan_path} if policy.scan_path is not None else {}
    44→    )
    45→    return build_work_queue(
    46→        state,
    47→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    48→            count=policy.count,
    49→            status="open",
    50→            include_subjective=policy.include_subjective,
    51→            subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state),
    52→            **scan_path_kwarg,
    53→        ),
    54→    )
    55→
    56→
    57→__all__ = ["OpenPlanQueuePolicy", "build_open_plan_queue"]
    58→
```

```
1→"""Finding prioritization/selection helpers for next items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanItem, PlanState
     6→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     7→    QueueBuildOptions,
     8→    build_work_queue,
     9→)
    10→
    11→
    12→def get_next_items(
    13→    state: PlanState,
    14→    tier: int | None = None,
    15→    count: int = 1,
    16→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    17→) -> list[PlanItem]:
    18→    """Get the N highest-priority open findings.
    19→
    20→    Legacy plan API intentionally returns only finding items (not synthetic
    21→    subjective queue items) so existing planner consumers stay stable.
    22→    The *tier* parameter is accepted for backward compatibility but ignored.
    23→    """
    24→    result = build_work_queue(
    25→        state,
    26→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    27→            count=count,
    28→            scan_path=scan_path,
    29→            status="open",
    30→            include_subjective=False,
    31→        ),
    32→    )
    33→    return [item for item in result["items"] if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
    34→
    35→
    36→def get_next_item(
    37→    state: PlanState,
    38→    tier: int | None = None,
    39→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    40→) -> PlanItem | None:
    41→    """Get the highest-priority open finding."""
    42→    items = get_next_items(state, tier=tier, count=1, scan_path=scan_path)
    43→    return items[0] if items else None
    44→
```

```
   179→def _plan_item_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   180→    """Build per-file sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   181→
   182→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   183→    raw_target = (
   184→        (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95)
   185→        if isinstance(state, dict)
   186→        else 95
   187→    )
   188→    try:
   189→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   190→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   191→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   192→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   193→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   194→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   195→
   196→    queue = build_work_queue(
   197→        queue_state,

   200→            status="open",
   201→            include_subjective=True,
   202→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   203→        ),
   204→    )

   206→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)

   209→
   210→    lines: list[str] = []
   211→    total_count = len(open_items)
   212→    if not open_items:
   213→        return lines
   214→
   215→    lines.extend([
   216→        "---",
   217→        f"## Open Items ({total_count})",
   218→        "",
   219→    ])

   222→        by_file.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   223→    )

   225→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   226→        lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   227→        lines.append("")
   228→        for item in file_items:
   229→            if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   230→                lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   231→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   232→                if item.get("primary_command"):
   233→                    lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   234→                continue
   235→
   236→            conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   237→            lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   238→            lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   239→        lines.append("")
   240→
   241→    return lines

   243→
   244→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
   245→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state.
   246→
   247→    When *plan* is provided (or auto-loaded from disk), user-ordered
   248→    items, clusters, skipped, and superseded sections are rendered.
   249→    When no plan exists, output is identical to the previous behavior.
   250→    """
   251→    findings = state["findings"]
   252→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   253→
   254→    # Auto-load plan if not provided
   255→    if plan is None:
   256→        try:
   257→            from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   258→            plan = load_plan()
   259→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   260→            plan = {}
   261→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   262→        plan = {}
   263→    plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
   264→    plan.setdefault("skipped", {})
   265→    plan.setdefault("clusters", {})
   266→
   267→    has_plan = bool(
   268→        plan
   269→        and (
   270→            plan.get("queue_order")
   271→            or plan.get("skipped")
   272→            or plan.get("clusters")
   273→        )
   274→    )
   275→
   276→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   277→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   278→    lines.extend(_summary_lines(stats))
   279→
   280→    if has_plan:
   281→        # Build full queue for item lookup
   282→        queue = build_work_queue(
   283→            state,
   284→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   285→                count=None,
   286→                status="open",
   287→                include_subjective=True,
   288→            ),
   289→        )
   290→        all_items = queue.get("items", [])
   291→        lines.extend(_plan_user_ordered_section(all_items, plan))
   292→
   293→        # Remaining: items NOT in queue_order or skipped
   294→        ordered_ids = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   295→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   296→        plan_ids = ordered_ids | skipped_ids
   297→        remaining = [item for item in all_items if item.get("id") not in plan_ids]
   298→        if remaining:
   299→            lines.append("---")
   300→            lines.append(f"## Remaining (mechanical order, {len(remaining)} items)")
   301→            lines.append("")
   302→
   303→        lines.extend(_plan_item_sections(findings, state=state))
   304→        lines.extend(_plan_skipped_section(all_items, plan))
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan workflow nudge.
     2→
     3→Dependency-light module that only imports plan/queue helpers — avoids
     4→the import cycle that previously forced function-level imports in scan.py.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_breakdown
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    12→
    13→
    14→def print_plan_workflow_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
    15→    """Print a queue-count reminder when plan-start scores exist."""
    16→    try:
    17→        plan = load_plan()
    18→        if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    19→            return
    20→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
    21→        queue_total = breakdown.actionable
    22→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    23→        return
    24→
    25→    if queue_total <= 0:
    26→        return
    27→    print(
    28→        colorize(
    29→            f"  Workflow: {queue_total} queue item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}."
    30→            " Score is frozen until the queue is clear — use `desloppify next` to begin.",
    31→            "dim",
    32→        )
    33→    )
    34→
```

```
1→"""Runtime state model for exclusions and in-memory caches."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from contextlib import contextmanager
     6→from contextvars import ContextVar
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→
    11→class FileTextCache:
    12→    """Optional read-through file-text cache used by scan/review passes."""
    13→
    14→    def __init__(self) -> None:
    15→        self._enabled = False
    16→        self._values: dict[str, str | None] = {}
    17→
    18→    def enable(self) -> None:
    19→        self._enabled = True
    20→        self._values.clear()
    21→
    22→    def disable(self) -> None:
    23→        self._enabled = False
    24→        self._values.clear()
    25→
    26→    def read(self, filepath: str) -> str | None:
    27→        if self._enabled and filepath in self._values:
    28→            return self._values[filepath]
    29→
    30→        try:
    31→            content = Path(filepath).read_text(errors="replace")
    32→        except OSError:
    33→            content = None
    34→        if self._enabled:
    35→            self._values[filepath] = content
    36→        return content
    37→
    38→
    39→class SourceFileCache:
    40→    """Small FIFO cache for source-file discovery results."""
    41→
    42→    def __init__(self, *, max_entries: int) -> None:
    43→        self.max_entries = max_entries
    44→        self.values: dict[tuple, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
    45→
    46→    def get(self, key: tuple) -> tuple[str, ...] | None:
    47→        return self.values.get(key)
    48→
    49→    def put(self, key: tuple, value: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
    50→        if len(self.values) >= self.max_entries:
    51→            self.values.pop(next(iter(self.values)))
    52→        self.values[key] = value
    53→
    54→    def clear(self) -> None:
    55→        self.values.clear()
    56→
    57→
    58→@dataclass
    59→class RuntimeContext:
    60→    """Mutable runtime container for exclusion and cache state."""
    61→
    62→    exclusions: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    63→    project_root: Path | None = None
    64→    query_file: Path | None = None
    65→    file_text_cache: FileTextCache = field(default_factory=FileTextCache)
    66→    cache_enabled: bool = False
    67→    source_file_cache: SourceFileCache = field(
    68→        default_factory=lambda: SourceFileCache(max_entries=16)
    69→    )
    70→
    71→
    72→_PROCESS_RUNTIME_CONTEXT = RuntimeContext()
    73→_RUNTIME_CONTEXT: ContextVar[RuntimeContext | None] = ContextVar(
    74→    "desloppify_runtime_context",
    75→    default=None,
    76→)
    77→
    78→
    79→def make_runtime_context(*, source_file_cache_max_entries: int = 16) -> RuntimeContext:
    80→    """Create an isolated runtime context."""
    81→    return RuntimeContext(
    82→        source_file_cache=SourceFileCache(max_entries=source_file_cache_max_entries)
    83→    )
    84→
    85→
    86→def current_runtime_context() -> RuntimeContext:
    87→    """Return the active runtime context (or process fallback)."""
    88→    runtime = _RUNTIME_CONTEXT.get()
    89→    if runtime is not None:
    90→        return runtime
    91→    return _PROCESS_RUNTIME_CONTEXT
    92→
    93→
    94→@contextmanager
    95→def runtime_scope(runtime: RuntimeContext | None = None):
```

## Implementation Plan: QueueContext Refactoring

### 1. Problem Analysis

After tracing every call site, here is the precise inconsistency map:

**Who calls `build_work_queue` directly?**

| Call site | passes `plan` | passes `policy` | has `target_strict` for policy |
|---|---|---|---|
| `next.py:_get_items` (line 198) | yes (as `plan_data`) | NO | NO (passes `subjective_threshold` only) |
| `queue_progress.py:plan_aware_queue_breakdown` (line 48) | yes (from caller) | NO | NO |
| `queue_guard.py:_check_queue_order_guard` (line 27) | yes | NO | NO |
| `plan/queue_render.py:cmd_plan_queue` (line 146) | yes | NO | NO |
| `show/scope.py:load_matches` (line 198) | NO | NO | NO |
| `planning/select.py:get_next_items` (line 24) | NO | NO | NO |
| `planning/render.py:_plan_item_sections` (line 196) | NO | NO | NO |
| `planning/render.py:generate_plan_md` (line 282) | NO | NO | NO |
| `planning/queue_policy.py:build_open_plan_queue` (line 45) | NO | NO | NO |

**Who calls `compute_subjective_visibility` directly?**

| Call site | passes `target_strict` | passes `plan` |
|---|---|---|
| `build_work_queue` (internal, line 113) | NO (uses default 95.0) | yes (from options) |
| `scan_workflow.py:_reconcile_plan_post_scan` (line 226) | YES | YES |
| `status_cmd.py:cmd_status` (line 117) | YES | YES |
| `preflight.py:_objective_and_subjective_backlog` (line 91) | NO (uses default 95.0) | YES |

**Who calls `plan_aware_queue_breakdown`?**

| Call site | passes `plan` |
|---|---|
| `scan_workflow.py:_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty` (line 193) | YES |
| `status_cmd.py:_print_score_section` (lines 192, 213) | YES |
| `next.py:_plan_queue_context` (line 157) | YES |
| `scan_preflight.py:scan_queue_preflight` (line 59) | YES |
| `plan_nudge.py:print_plan_workflow_nudge` (line 20) | YES |
| `scan_workflow.py:print_execution_or_reveal` (line 311) | YES |

The core bug class: `build_work_queue` internally calls `compute_subjective_visibility` **without** `target_strict`, because `QueueBuildOptions` does not carry it. The `subjective_threshold` field on `QueueBuildOptions` controls the threshold for *which subjective items to surface* (a filter), not the policy's `target_strict` (which controls which dimensions are "under target"). These are different parameters that happen to often be the same value.

### 2. Design Decisions

**Q1: Where does `QueueContext` live?**

In `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/context.py` -- a new file in the engine layer. This is the right level because:
- It needs to be importable by both `_work_queue/core.py` and app-layer commands
- It is a data container with a factory function, not a command-layer concern
- It follows the existing pattern where `_work_queue/` houses queue-related data types

**Q2: What fields does it have?**

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueContext:
    """Resolved, frozen context for queue operations.
    
    Created once per command invocation. Carries all parameters needed to
    build consistent queue counts and subjective visibility policy.
    """
    state: StateModel
    plan: dict | None = None
    target_strict: float = 95.0
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None  # lazily computed on first access

    @cached_property  # not possible on frozen dataclass, see below
    ...
```

Actually, since this is a frozen dataclass and we want lazy policy computation, the better design is: the factory function eagerly computes the policy. This matches `scan_workflow.py`'s established pattern of "compute policy once, reuse."

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueContext:
    """Resolved, frozen context for all queue count operations.

    Created once per command invocation via ``queue_context()``.
    Carries pre-resolved plan, target_strict, and subjective visibility
    policy so every queue operation within a single command sees
    identical parameters.
    """
    plan: dict | None
    target_strict: float
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility
```

That is it. Three fields. The state is NOT on QueueContext -- it is passed separately (it is large, mutable, and already threaded everywhere). The QueueContext only carries the parameters that different call sites were inconsistently computing.

**Q3: What is the factory function signature?**

```python
def queue_context(
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    config: dict | None = None,
    plan: dict | None = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    target_strict: float | None = None,
) -> QueueContext:
```

Where:
- `config` defaults to None (meaning "use 95.0 for target_strict")
- `plan` defaults to a sentinel that means "auto-load from disk" (matching current behavior)
- `target_strict` explicitly overrides the config-derived value when provided

The factory:
1. Resolves plan: loads from disk if sentinel, uses None if no plan file
2. Resolves target_strict: from config if provided, else fallback 95.0
3. Computes policy: `compute_subjective_visibility(state, target_strict=..., plan=...)`
4. Returns frozen QueueContext

**Q4: How does it integrate with QueueBuildOptions?**

`QueueBuildOptions` gains one new optional field: `context: QueueContext | None = None`. When set, `build_work_queue` extracts `plan` and `policy` from it instead of from the individual fields. The existing `plan` and `policy` fields on `QueueBuildOptions` remain for backward compatibility and are ignored when `context` is provided.

This means:
- All existing code continues to work unchanged
- New code passes `context=ctx` and does not need to set `plan=` or `policy=`
- `build_work_queue` resolves the policy from context.policy (already computed with correct target_strict) instead of calling `compute_subjective_visibility` internally with wrong defaults

**Q5: What changes at each call site?**

Phase 1 (add QueueContext, no call site changes):
- Create `_work_queue/context.py` with `QueueContext` and `queue_context()`
- Add `context` field to `QueueBuildOptions`
- Update `build_work_queue` to respect `context` field
- Re-export from `engine/work_queue.py` facade

Phase 2 (migrate high-value call sites):
- `next.py`: Create `ctx = queue_context(state, config=config)` once, pass it via `QueueBuildOptions(context=ctx, ...)` and also into `_plan_queue_context()`
- `status_cmd.py`: Create `ctx` once, use it for both `plan_aware_queue_breakdown` and `compute_subjective_visibility` calls
- `scan_workflow.py`: Already correct (computes policy once), but can adopt QueueContext in `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty` and `print_execution_or_reveal`
- `preflight.py`: Use `queue_context()` instead of bare `compute_subjective_visibility()` to get target_strict
- `queue_guard.py`: Pass `ctx` into `build_work_queue`
- `plan/queue_render.py`: Pass `ctx` into `build_work_queue`

Phase 3 (migrate indirect callers):
- `plan_aware_queue_breakdown`: Gains optional `context: QueueContext | None = None` parameter; when set, passes it through to `build_work_queue`
- `plan_nudge.py`: Creates ctx, passes to `plan_aware_queue_breakdown`
- `scan_preflight.py`: Creates ctx, passes to `plan_aware_queue_breakdown`

**Q6: What about `plan_aware_queue_breakdown` -- does it take QueueContext?**

Yes. Its signature becomes:

```python
def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    state: dict,
    plan: dict | None = None,
    *,
    context: QueueContext | None = None,
) -> QueueBreakdown:
```

When `context` is provided:
- `plan` parameter is ignored (context.plan is used)
- `build_work_queue` is called with `QueueBuildOptions(context=context, ...)`

When `context` is not provided, the current behavior is preserved (backward compatible).

**Q7: How does preflight simplify?**

Currently in `preflight.py:_objective_and_subjective_backlog`:

```python

    plan_data = load_plan()
    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, plan=plan_data)
    # missing target_strict!
    objective_total = policy.objective_count
    return objective_total, 0
```

After:

```python
def _objective_and_subjective_backlog(state, *, blocking_dims):
    ctx = queue_context(state)  # auto-loads plan, uses default target_strict
    objective_total = ctx.policy.objective_count
    return objective_total, 0
```

This is already better because `queue_context()` auto-loads the plan and uses a consistent target_strict. But ideally, preflight would accept a QueueContext from above so the caller controls the config:

```python
def review_rerun_preflight(state, args, *, state_file=None, save_fn=save_state):
    ...
    ctx = queue_context(state, config=load_config())
    ...
    objective_total, subjective_total = _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
        state, blocking_dims=blocking_dims, context=ctx,
    )
```

2. **Add `context` field to `QueueBuildOptions`** in `_work_queue/core.py`

### 3. Detailed File Changes

**New file: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/context.py`**

```python
"""QueueContext: frozen, pre-resolved parameters for consistent queue operations."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    SubjectiveVisibility,
    compute_subjective_visibility,
)
from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel

_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD = object()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueContext:
    """Pre-resolved context for queue count operations.

    Created once per command invocation via ``queue_context()``.
    Ensures all queue operations within a single command see identical
    plan, target_strict, and subjective visibility policy.
    """
    plan: dict | None
    target_strict: float
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility


def queue_context(
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    config: dict | None = None,
    plan: dict | None | object = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    target_strict: float | None = None,
) -> QueueContext:
    """Build a frozen QueueContext from the current runtime.

    Parameters
    ----------
    state : StateModel
        Current application state (findings, scores, etc.).
    config : dict, optional
        Application config. Used to derive ``target_strict`` when not
        explicitly provided.
    plan : dict or None, optional
        Living plan dict. Defaults to auto-loading from disk. Pass
        ``None`` explicitly to skip plan loading.
    target_strict : float, optional
        Override for the target strict score threshold. When not
        provided, reads from ``config["target_strict_score"]`` with
        fallback 95.0.
    """
    # Resolve plan
    if plan is _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD:
        from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
        from desloppify.engine._plan.persistence import load_plan
        try:
            resolved_plan = load_plan()
        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
            resolved_plan = None
    else:
        resolved_plan = plan  # type: ignore[assignment]

    # Resolve target_strict
    if target_strict is not None:
        resolved_target = target_strict
    elif config is not None:
        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
        resolved_target = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    else:
        resolved_target = 95.0

    # Compute policy with all parameters resolved
    resolved_policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
        state,
        target_strict=resolved_target,
        plan=resolved_plan,
    )

    return QueueContext(
        plan=resolved_plan,
        target_strict=resolved_target,
        policy=resolved_policy,
    )
```

In `build_work_queue`, after resolving options, extract from context:

```python
def build_work_queue(state, *, options=None):
    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    
    # When QueueContext is provided, use its pre-resolved plan and policy.
    ctx = resolved_options.context
    effective_plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else resolved_options.plan
    
    # ... later, in policy resolution ...
    policy = (
        (ctx.policy if ctx is not None else None)
        or resolved_options.policy
        or compute_subjective_visibility(state, plan=effective_plan)
    )
```

And substitute `effective_plan` for `resolved_options.plan` throughout the function body. This is a minimal, non-breaking change.

### 4. What This Fixes

1. **Missing `plan` parameter**: `queue_context()` auto-loads plan, so callers that forget get it automatically.
2. **Missing `target_strict`**: `queue_context()` derives target_strict from config, so the policy always has the correct threshold. This fixes the preflight bug and the build_work_queue internal policy computation bug.
3. **Circular subjective blocking in preflight**: Already fixed by the existing code (returns 0 for subjective_total), but QueueContext ensures the objective_count is computed with the right target_strict.
4. **Workflow items counted inconsistently**: Not directly fixed by QueueContext (already addressed by `QueueBreakdown.actionable`), but QueueContext ensures the breakdown is computed with consistent parameters.

### 5. What NOT To Change

- `scan_workflow.py:_reconcile_plan_post_scan` -- This function already correctly computes policy once with target_strict and reuses it. It could adopt QueueContext but the benefit is low and the risk of regression in scan merging is high. Leave it for a follow-up.
- `planning/render.py`, `planning/select.py`, `planning/queue_policy.py` -- These are internal plan-rendering modules that operate in markdown-generation context. They do not contribute to the user-facing "how much work remains?" counting problem. Migrate later if desired.
- `show/scope.py:load_matches` -- This uses `include_subjective=False` and does not count queue totals. It does not need plan/policy.
